2. NET UNIVERSE: CORE PHILOSOPHY

2.1 THE MISSION

NET's Purpose:



"We don't teach English. We build English speakers."

The Problem NET Solves:



Vietnamese adults study English for 10+ years

They can read and write (textbook knowledge)

But they can't speak (confidence deficit)

Traditional education traps them in "Grammar Prison"

NET's Solution:



Natural acquisition (like learning Vietnamese)

Confidence first, grammar second

1 hour daily practice (not disruptive)

Community support (Buddy system, monthly events)

Guaranteed transformation in 90 days

2.2 THE METAPHOR SYSTEM

ELEPHANT IN CHAINS

Story: Vietnamese students are like elephants in chains. From childhood, they're told "Don't speak until perfect." These invisible chains hold them back even as adults.

NET's Role: Break the chains. Show them they're free.

MOUSE → TIGER TRANSFORMATION

MOUSE VOICE:



Quiet, scared, apologizes constantly

Translates mentally before speaking

Studies grammar, never practices speaking

Freezes around native speakers

TIGER VOICE:



Loud, confident, owns mistakes

Thinks in English directly

Practices speaking first, grammar later

Approaches foreigners without fear

NET's Job: Transform every Mouse into a Tiger.

GRAMMAR PRISON

Prison: Traditional education system



Locks students in grammar rules

Shames mistakes

Tests memorization, not communication

Forces perfection before speaking

Escape: NET methodology



Natural conversation from Day 1

Mistakes celebrated as learning data

Real communication over textbook accuracy

PHOENIX REBIRTH

Metaphor: Learning English with NET is a rebirth



Old scared self burns away

New confident speaker emerges

Transformation is permanent

You can never un-learn confidence

2.3 THE PSYCHOLOGICAL INSIGHT

KEY INSIGHT: Fear, not ability, is the barrier.

Vietnamese students have the cognitive capacity to speak English. What's missing is:



Permission to make mistakes (shame-based education broke this)

Daily practice habit (traditional school didn't build this)

Supportive community (learning was isolated, not social)

Identity shift (they see themselves as "learners," not "speakers")

NET addresses all four.

3. THE FOUR PILLARS: PEDAGOGY

3.1 PILLAR 1: LISTEN & UNDERSTAND

Principle: Massive comprehensible input

The Rule: 60% comprehension



<60% = too hard (frustration, noise)

60-80% = optimal (flow state, learning)



90% = too easy (boredom, no growth)

Daily Practice:



45 minutes of English content (movies, podcasts, YouTube)

Student chooses topics they ENJOY (critical for consistency)

Subtitles okay initially, wean off over time

In Class:



Teacher speaks 70%+ English (beginners) to 90%+ (advanced)

Use visuals, gestures, context (not translation)

Repeat key phrases in multiple contexts

3.2 PILLAR 2: SPEAK & MIMIC

Principle: Output is the engine of fluency

The Method:



Listen to phrase → Repeat exactly (intonation, rhythm, emotion)

Don't analyze grammar — just copy

Build muscle memory in mouth and brain

Daily Practice:



15 minutes shadow-speaking

Record self, compare to native speaker

Focus on sounds, not perfection

In Class:



Choral repetition (whole class speaks together)

Pair practice (drills with partner)

Solo performance (builds confidence under pressure)

Volume = Confidence: Tiger Voice is loud. Always encourage louder speech.

3.3 PILLAR 3: LEARN IN CONTEXT

Principle: Real sentences stick. Word lists don't.

The Rule: Never teach vocabulary in isolation

WRONG:



"Sarcasm means... irony or... mocking tone..."

RIGHT:



"Oh great, another meeting. Just what I needed today."

[Student hears tone, sees eye roll, feels the sarcasm]

Always Show:



3-5 example sentences per concept

Real usage in authentic context

Emotional association (funny, shocking, relatable)

3.4 PILLAR 4: BUILD CONFIDENCE

Principle: Fear is the #1 barrier. Confidence comes before perfection.

The Method:



Celebrate mistakes ("Great! You tried!")

Never shame errors

Praise courage over accuracy

Create safe environment

Systems:



Buddy System: Weekly peer practice (reduces pressure)

Monthly Events: Community building (you're not alone)

Tiger Voice Training: Physical confidence through volume

CRITICAL: Mistakes are data, not shame.

3.5 THE 60% RULE (CRITICAL)

This is the master law of NET pedagogy.

If student understands:



<60% of content: TOO HARD → Go easier

60-80% of content: PERFECT → Stay here (flow state)

>90% of content: TOO EASY → Go harder

Why this matters:



Below 60% = Noise wins, brain shuts down

60-80% = Brain fills gaps naturally, optimal learning

Above 90% = No challenge, no growth

Your job as architect: Design lessons that hit 60-80% for each student level.

6. SALES SYSTEM: CONVERSION

6.1 SALES PHILOSOPHY

"Diagnose & Prescribe, not Push & Pressure"

NET sales is consultative, not transactional.

Process:



Listen: Understand student's real problem

Diagnose: Identify root cause (fear, plateau, time, etc.)

Prescribe: Recommend right solution (program, intensity, format)

Support: Guide decision, don't pressure

6.2 ASSESSMENT PROTOCOL (30-45 MIN)

PART 1: Grammar Test (10 min)

20 multiple-choice questions (A1-C1)

Auto-scored, determines base level

PART 2: Listening Test (10 min)

3 audio clips, comprehension questions

Tests 60% rule

PART 3: Speaking Test (15 min)

5 prompts, 1-2 min each, recorded

Evaluates fluency, pronunciation, confidence

PROMPTS:



"Tell me about yourself"

"Describe your typical workday"

"What's your favorite hobby?"

"Tell about a time speaking English was difficult"

"Why do you want to improve English?"

PART 4: Diagnostic Conversation (10 min)

Discuss results

Identify Mouse vs Tiger archetype

Understand motivation and goals

6.3 OBJECTION HANDLING (TOP 10)

OBJECTION 1: "I've tried classes before. They didn't work."REBUTTAL: Traditional classes teach grammar. NET builds confidence. Different method, different results. 80% of our students complete the program (industry average: 30%).

OBJECTION 2: "I don't have time."REBUTTAL: NET is 1 hour a day, not 3-hour sessions. Morning or evening. Online or in-person. Fits your life. You're already wasting time being held back by English.

OBJECTION 3: "I'm too shy to speak."REBUTTAL: That's Mouse Voice. NET transforms Mouse → Tiger. You practice in supportive environment. Mistakes are celebrated. 90% of shy students gain confidence within 30 days.

OBJECTION 4: "It's expensive."REBUTTAL: How much have you spent on classes that didn't work? How many years have you lost? NET delivers results in 90 days. Payment plans available. If you complete the program, guaranteed results. Can your current method say that?

OBJECTION 5: "I'm too old to learn."REBUTTAL: Myth. Your brain can learn language at any age using natural acquisition. Most students are 25-45. Age isn't the barrier. Fear is. NET removes fear.

OBJECTION 6: "I need to pass IELTS/TOEIC."REBUTTAL: NET builds real fluency — foundation for any exam. Once you speak naturally, test prep is easier. We also offer exam modules. But here's the truth: What's more valuable — a test score or actual communication ability?

OBJECTION 7: "Can I just do online?"REBUTTAL: Yes! We have online classes (Zoom), same quality. Some students prefer in-person (Lakeview), some prefer online flexibility. Your choice. Same curriculum, same results.

OBJECTION 8: "What if I don't improve?"REBUTTAL: 80% completion rate. 90% report breakthrough confidence. If you attend classes and do daily practice, you WILL improve. We track progress monthly. If you're not seeing results, we adjust strategy. Risk-free.

OBJECTION 9: "I need to start next month."REBUTTAL: Understood. The next cohort starts [date]. Can I reserve your spot? We're capping at 10 students per class (quality over quantity). Don't want you to miss out.

OBJECTION 10: "Let me think about it."REBUTTAL: Of course! What specific concerns do you have? [address directly] Here's what I suggest: Come to one trial class, no commitment. Experience NET teaching. Then decide. Fair?

7. TEACHING SYSTEM: TRANSFORMATION

7.1 LESSON STRUCTURE: 10-5-5-3

Every 90-minute lesson follows this pattern:



10 Core Vocabulary words (in context, never isolated)

5 Idioms (real usage, cultural context)

5 Slang expressions (modern, authentic)

3 Games (Warm-up, Mid-lesson, Final challenge)

Timing Breakdown:



Warm-up (10 min)

Input (5 min) — present new content

Guided Practice (5 min) — choral repetition, drills

Free Practice (60 min) — conversation, role-plays

Feedback (10 min) — corrections, praise

7.2 THE 60 LESSON TOPICS

CATEGORY 1: PRAGMATICS (12 topics)

Sarcasm, Funny vs Funny, Understatement, Reading Between Lines

Vague Time, Passive Aggression, "Interesting", Cultural References

Taboo Topics, Compliments, Apologies, Small Talk

CATEGORY 2: WORKPLACE (12 topics)

Daily Standup, Polite Disagreeing, Interrupting, Bad News

Asking for Promotion, Explaining Bugs, Chasing Emails, Feedback

Difficult Coworkers, Networking, Phone Calls, Presentations

CATEGORY 3: SOCIAL (10 topics)

Ordering Coffee, Grocery Shopping, Doctor, Renting

Making Friends, Dating, Traveling, Emergencies, Complaining, Parties

CATEGORY 4: TECH & GAMING (8 topics)

Gaming Lingo, Tech Jargon, Social Media, AI & Chatbots

Cryptocurrency, Streaming, Memes, Remote Work

CATEGORY 5: EMOTIONS (6 topics)

Anxiety & Stress, Confidence vs Arrogance, Motivation

Relationships, Cultural Identity, Imposter Syndrome

CATEGORY 6: GRAMMAR (6 topics)

Conditionals, Passive Voice, Reported Speech

Modals, Relative Clauses, Phrasal Verbs

CATEGORY 7: PRONUNCIATION (4 topics)

TH Sounds, R vs L, V vs W, Word Stress

CATEGORY 8: BUSINESS (2 topics)

Negotiations, Professional Emails

TOTAL: 60 topics = 80 lesson templates (some topics split into multiple lessons)

7.3 CRISIS PROTOCOLS (10 SCRIPTS)

CRISIS #1: THE SILENCESituation: No one speaks when you ask a questionScript: "Everyone stand up! Popcorn Story. I start, then point to someone. 'Yesterday I went to the market and I saw...' Nhan, continue!"

CRISIS #2: ONE STUDENT DOMINATESSituation: Same person answers everythingScript: "Kiet, I love your energy! Now I need to hear from others. Everyone who HASN'T spoken, raise your hand. [Call on them]"

CRISIS #3: "I DON'T KNOW"Situation: Student gives up without tryingScript: "That's okay! What's your BEST GUESS? Mouse says 'I don't know.' Tiger says 'Let me try.' Go!"

CRISIS #4: STUDENT CRIESSituation: Emotional breakdown (frustration, stress)Script: "Hey, it's okay. Let's step outside together. [Privately] What's going on? English is hard, I get it. But you're doing great. Let's take a break."

CRISIS #5: TECH FAILURESituation: Zoom crashes, mic diesScript: "Tech is difficult today. No problem! Everyone grab pen and paper. Writing exercise while I fix this. [Assign task]"

CRISIS #6: RUDE STUDENTSituation: Interrupts, mocks, challenges teacherScript (In Class): "Let's pause. I need everyone's respect. If you have feedback, we can talk after class."Script (After Class): "What happened? That wasn't like you. I need you to respect this space. Can we agree?"

CRISIS #7: STUDENTS ARE LOSTSituation: Content too hard, blank staresScript: "I see confusion. Let me back up. This is too hard. Let's go simpler. [Repeat with basic English, gestures] Does this make more sense?"

CRISIS #8: RUNNING OUT OF TIMESituation: 10 min left, haven't finished lessonScript: "Short on time. The most important thing today is [X]. Let's spend last 10 minutes nailing that. [Cut non-essentials]"

CRISIS #9: CAN'T ANSWER QUESTIONSituation: Student asks something you don't knowScript: "Great question! Honestly, I don't know off the top of my head. Let me look it up for next class. In meantime, does anyone else know? [If no] Perfect homework — everyone research this."

CRISIS #10: STUDENT WANTS TO QUITSituation: Student says they're dropping outScript: "I hear you. English is frustrating. Before you decide, let's talk. What's making you want to quit? [Listen] Progress isn't linear. You're doing better than you think. Give it 2 more weeks. If you still feel this way, we'll talk again. Deal?"

7.4 BUDDY SYSTEM

Purpose: Weekly 1-hour peer practice outside class

Pairing Strategy:



Similar level (prevents domination)

Opposite archetypes (Tiger pulls Mouse forward)

Shared interests (gaming, tech, business)

First Session (Guided):



Teacher provides 10 conversation topics

5 min each topic, switch speaker/listener

Report back: "Did you meet your buddy?"

Ongoing:



Students self-direct (own topics, format)

Meet at café, park, online

NET PET tracks attendance

Benefits:



Reduces pressure (peer vs teacher)

Builds friendships

+1 hour/week practice

90% retention rate (vs 60% solo)

8. PRODUCT SYSTEMS: TECHNOLOGY

8.1 PHOENIX ENGINE: SECURITY

Phoenix Engine is NET's security framework (P0-P5).



P0: BASELINE SECURITY (MANDATORY)

No Inline Event Handlers: Never use onclick=, use addEventListener

Input Sanitization: All user input escaped before processing

CSP Headers: Content Security Policy blocks unauthorized scripts

HTTPS Only: All traffic encrypted, no HTTP

Session Management: Secure tokens, httpOnly cookies, auto-timeout

P0 is non-negotiable. Violation = no deploy.

P1: DATA PROTECTION (MANDATORY FOR USER DATA)

Encryption at Rest: Passwords (bcrypt), PII (AES-256)

Encryption in Transit: TLS 1.2+ for all data transmission

PII Handling: Minimal collection, secure storage, GDPR compliance

Audit Logging: Log all security events (logins, changes, access)

P2: ATTACK PREVENTION (MANDATORY FOR PUBLIC APPS)

Rate Limiting: Prevent brute-force and DDoS

CSRF Protection: Tokens for state-changing requests

SQL Injection Prevention: Parameterized queries only

XSS Prevention: Escape all user content before rendering

P3-P5: ADVANCED (ENTERPRISE ONLY)

P3: GDPR compliance, SOC 2 certification, penetration testing

P4: Real-time threat detection, anomaly detection, security dashboards

P5: Zero-trust architecture, micro-segmentation, least-privilege access

4. PHOENIX ENGINE: SECURITY PROTOCOLS
4.1 P0: BASELINE (NON-NEGOTIABLE)
You simply do not write code that violates P0.
NO INLINE EVENTS:
❌ <button onclick="doThing()">
✅ button.addEventListener('click', doThing)
INPUT SANITIZATION:
Never trust `req.body`.
Always escape output before rendering to DOM (prevent XSS).
Use parameterized queries for SQL (prevent Injection).
HTTPS ONLY:
Force redirect to HTTPS in Express middleware.
SECURE HEADERS:
Implement Helmet.js or manual headers:
Content-Security-Policy
X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff
X-Frame-Options: DENY
4.2 DATA PROTECTION
Passwords: MUST use bcrypt (salt rounds >= 10). NEVER store plain text.
Tokens: JWT with httpOnly cookie. Never store JWT in localStorage (XSS risk).
PII: Minimal collection. If not needed, delete it.
4.3 AUDIT TRIGGER
If you see code that violates Phoenix P0:
1. STOP.
2. FLAG as "CRITICAL SECURITY RISK".
3. REWRITE immediately.
5. NET PET: LOGIC & GAMIFICATION
5.1 XP CALCULATION LOGIC
// Base Values
const XP_RATES = {
  LISTENING_SESSION: 10, // per 45 min
  SPEAKING_SESSION: 10,  // per 15 min
  CLASS_ATTENDANCE: 50,
  BUDDY_SESSION: 30,
  MONTHLY_EVENT: 100
};

// Logic
function calculateDailyXP(activities) {
  let total = 0;
  activities.forEach(act => {
    total += XP_RATES[act.type] || 0;
  });
  return total;
}
5.2 STREAK LOGIC
Rule: Must complete at least 1 "Practice" activity within 24 hours.
Reset: If > 24 hours since last activity, streak = 0.
Milestones: [7, 30, 90, 365].
5.3 MOOD TRACKER API
Endpoint: POST /api/mood
Payload: { userId, mood: 'happy'|'neutral'|'sad', note: string }
Logic:
If mood === 'sad':
  Check last 2 days.
  If last 2 days also 'sad':
    Trigger Alert("CRISIS_RISK", userId).

Based on "Teaching Bible: Category 2 (Workplace) & 4 (Tech)", the focus is:

NET-CASE-MANAGER-LINH: SYSTEM UPDATE

PRIORITY: CRITICAL

MODE: DEEP FORENSIC AUDIT (x5 EXPANSION)

TARGET: NET_MASTER_SEED_V5.js

Mr. Hobbs, this is the Deep Diagnostic Audit for Student Linh. It moves beyond simple error correction into Linguistic Forensics and Psychometric Profiling.

This data is formatted to be the foundational "Truth Source" for her entire NET journey. It explains not just what she is doing wrong, but why her brain is doing it, and exactly how the NET System will dismantle these habits.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

SECTION 1: LINGUISTIC FORENSICS (THE "WHY" BEHIND THE ERRORS)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

1.1 THE "NULL COPULA" SYNDROME (The Missing Verb)

The Artifact: "I CEO," "My Mother Thum," "Because good."

The Root Cause (L1 Interference): In Vietnamese, adjectival predicates do not require the verb "to be" (Cô ấy đẹp = She beautiful). She is mapping this rule onto English. She views "am/is/are" as optional clutter.

NET Classification: Structural Blindness (Type A). She does not "see" the verb as necessary for meaning, so her brain filters it out before speech.

1.2 THE "TOPIC-COMMENT" STRUCTURE

The Artifact: "My Family six people."

The Root Cause: She uses a "Topic-Comment" syntax typical of East Asian languages (Gia đình tôi [Topic] + sáu người [Comment]). She is bypassing the English Subject-Verb-Object (SVO) requirement ("My family HAS six people" or "There ARE six people").

NET Risk: If uncorrected, this calcifies into "Pidgin English" where she just throws nouns at people until they understand.

1.3 VOCABULARY DISSOCIATION

The Artifact: "I AGC [Agency?] and singer."

The Analysis: She knows the concept (Actor/Agency) but lacks the phonetic retrieval speed, so she writes abbreviations or near-misses. She is "guessing" at the spelling based on incomplete hearing.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

SECTION 2: PHONOLOGICAL TOPOGRAPHY (THE MOUTH)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

2.1 THE "OPEN SYLLABLE" CONSTRAINT

Evidence: "Peo-po" (People), "Si" (Six), "Nai" (Nice).

Mechanism: Vietnamese words cannot end in sounds like /s/, /l/, /ks/, /z/. Her mouth physically stops before the word is finished because her muscle memory says "Stop here."

Impact: This destroys her Pluralization and Tense markers (Past tense 'ed', Plural 's'). Even if she knows the grammar rule, her mouth deletes the evidence.

2.2 THE ROBOT RHYTHM (Isosyllabic Stress)

Evidence: "U-En-Ter-Tain-Ment" (Equal stress on all syllables).

Mechanism: She speaks English with Vietnamese tones and timing (Syllable-Timed Language). She does not reduce unstressed vowels (the "schwa").

NET Goal: We must teach her English Swing (Stress-Timed Language). She needs to learn to "eat" the unimportant words.

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

SECTION 3: PSYCHOMETRIC PROFILE (THE MIND)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

3.1 THE "CEO EGO" PARADOX

Status: High (CEO/Singer).

Competence: Low (E1).

The Friction: She is accustomed to commanding a room. In English, she feels like a child. This creates a massive Affective Filter (Anxiety Wall).

Behavioral Prediction: She will be impatient with "basic" drills (colors, numbers) because they feel demeaning. She will try to rush to complex sentences ("I negotiate contract") before she can say "I am hungry."

3.2 LEARNING STYLE: "THE ARCHITECT"

She writes lists. She categorizes ("Sentence - Word").

Strategy: Do not just "talk" to her. Give her formulas. She needs to see the "Blueprint" of the sentence (S + V + O) to feel safe. She is analytical, not intuitive.

SECTION 5: THE TACTICAL ROADMAP (PHASE 1)

═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

WEEKS 1-4: "REBUILDING THE FOUNDATION"

1. THE "TO BE" BOOTCAMP (The Glue)

We cannot let her say another sentence without a verb.

The Drill: "Are you a singer?" -> "Yes, I AM a singer." (Not just "Yes").

The Pivot: "Is he a singer?" -> "No, he IS a manager."

2. THE "ENDING SOUND" CONTRACT

She must agree that a word is not finished until the sound is finished.

Visual Aid: Use hand gestures. When she says "Nice," hold your hand up until she makes the "ssss" sound, then close your hand.

Target Words: Six, Boss, House, Is, Because.

3. THE VOCABULARY RESCUE

She wrote "AGC". We need to fix her professional vocab.

Fix: Agency, Acting, Performance, Director.

Method: She reads these words -> You correct pronunciation -> She uses them in "I am..." sentences.

        :root {

        }

            font-weight: 900;

            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

            font-size: 0.75rem;
            color: var(--purple);

            border-radius: 4px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 15px;

        /* SECTIONS */
        .card {
            background: var(--glass);

        :root {

        }

        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }

        .bg-glow {

        h2 { font-size: 2rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: 800; }
        
        .gradient-text {

        .pillar {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            padding: 25px;

        /* --- PRICING --- */

</head>

        h1 { font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 5vw, 4rem); font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: -1px; line-height: 1.1; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        h2 { font-size: 1.8rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: 800; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 15px; }
        h3 { font-size: 1.1rem; color: var(--gold); font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px; margin-bottom: 15px; }
        
        .tag {

        }

        .vi {

        /* --- CARDS --- */
        .glass-panel {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: 20px;
            padding: 35px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
        }

        /* --- SKILL BARS (THE SCORE) --- */

        .skill-track { height: 10px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius: 5px; overflow: hidden; }
        .skill-fill { height: 100%; border-radius: 5px; transition: width 1s ease; }
        
        .fill-red { background: var(--danger); width: 10%; box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--danger); } /* 1/10 */
        .fill-orange { background: #f97316; width: 20%; } /* 2/10 */

    </style>

        }
        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.2); }

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET Dashboard: Linh</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444: INTERACTIVE DASHBOARD
           UX: TABBED NAVIGATION | LANGUAGE: SIMPLE E1
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --purple: #a855f7;
            --gold: #f59e0b;
            --dark: #0f0f1a;
            --panel: #1a1a2e;
            --text: #ffffff;
            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);
            --danger: #ef4444;
            --warning: #f97316;
            --success: #10b981;
        }

        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--dark);
            color: var(--text);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            line-height: 1.5;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            padding: 20px;
        }

        .dashboard-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 900px;
            background: rgba(20, 20, 35, 0.8);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
            border-radius: 24px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(20px);
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        header {
            padding: 30px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            text-align: center;
        }
        h1 { font-size: 2rem; font-weight: 800; margin-bottom: 5px; }
        .status-badge {
            display: inline-block;
            background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15);
            color: var(--danger);
            padding: 5px 12px;
            border-radius: 50px;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            border: 1px solid var(--danger);
        }

        /* --- TABS --- */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            overflow-x: auto;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
            padding: 10px;
            gap: 10px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        
        .tab-btn {
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            padding: 12px 20px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 600;
            font-family: 'Inter';
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            white-space: nowrap;
            transition: 0.2s;
        }
        
        .tab-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }
        
        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
        }

        /* --- CONTENT AREA --- */
        .content-area {
            padding: 40px;
            min-height: 400px;
        }

        .tab-content { display: none; animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease; }
        .tab-content.active { display: block; }

        @keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- METRICS & VISUALS --- */
        .score-big { font-size: 4rem; font-weight: 900; line-height: 1; margin-bottom: 10px; }
        .score-red { color: var(--danger); }
        .score-orange { color: var(--warning); }
        .score-gold { color: var(--gold); }

        .vi {
            display: block;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-style: italic;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            border-left: 2px solid var(--gold);
            padding-left: 10px;
            margin-top: 5px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        .box {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }

        /* --- OFFER --- */
        .price-tag { font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--gold); text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }
        .cta-btn {
            display: block; width: 100%; text-align: center;
            background: var(--gold); color: #000;
            padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px;
            font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; margin-top: 20px;
            transition: 0.2s;
        }
        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

            </p>
            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px;">
                <div class="box" style="text-align: center;">
                    <div class="score-big score-red">15%</div>
                    <div>Current Skill</div>
                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; text-align: center;">Kỹ năng hiện tại</span>
                </div>
                <div class="box" style="text-align: center;">
                    <div class="score-big score-gold">90%</div>
                    <div>Potential</div>
                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; text-align: center;">Tiềm năng phát triển</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="listening" class="tab-content">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                <h2>Listening (Nghe)</h2>
                <div class="score-big score-red" style="font-size: 2.5rem;">2/10</div>
            </div>
            
            <div class="box">
                <strong>Problem: The "Gap"</strong>
                <p>You only hear 1 or 2 words in a sentence. You guess the rest. If you guess wrong, you panic.</p>
                <span class="vi">Bạn chỉ nghe được 1-2 từ khóa và đoán phần còn lại. Nếu đoán sai, bạn sẽ hoảng loạn.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="box">
                <strong>Why? (Nguyên nhân)</strong>
                <p>English has ending sounds (s, t, k). Vietnamese does not. Your ears are deleting these sounds.</p>
                <span class="vi">Tai của bạn đang tự động xóa bỏ các âm cuối của tiếng Anh (s, t, k) vì tiếng Việt không có chúng.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="speaking" class="tab-content">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                <h2>Speaking (Nói)</h2>
                <div class="score-big score-red" style="font-size: 2.5rem;">1/10</div>
            </div>

            <div class="box">
                <strong>Problem: No Sentence Structure</strong>
                <p>You speak in single words. "Me... go... home." You do not connect them.</p>
                <span class="vi">Bạn chỉ nói từ đơn lẻ. Không kết nối thành câu hoàn chỉnh.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="box">
                <strong>Critical Error: The "To Be" Verb</strong>
                <p>You say: "She happy." (Vietnamese logic)<br>Correct: "She <strong>IS</strong> happy." (English logic)</p>
                <span class="vi">Lỗi thiếu động từ "To Be". Bạn đang dùng logic tiếng Việt để nói tiếng Anh.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="readwrite" class="tab-content">
            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px;">
                <div>

                <div>
                    <h3>Writing (Viết)</h3>

            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="mindset" class="tab-content">
            <h2>Psychology (Tâm Lý)</h2>
            
            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--success);">

                <span class="vi">Động lực cao. Bạn đã sẵn sàng để thay đổi.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--danger);">

                <span class="vi">Bạn sợ sai. Bạn nói lí nhí vì sợ người khác nghe thấy lỗi của mình.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="plan" class="tab-content">

            <div class="box">

            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

</div>

<script>
    function openTab(tabName) {
        // Hide all tabs
        var contents = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-content");
        for (var i = 0; i < contents.length; i++) {
            contents[i].classList.remove("active");
        }
        
        // Deactivate all buttons
        var btns = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-btn");
        for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {
            btns[i].classList.remove("active");
        }

        // Show selected tab
        document.getElementById(tabName).classList.add("active");
        
        // Activate button
        event.currentTarget.classList.add("active");
    }
</script>

</body>
</html>

    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444 AESTHETIC ENGINE
           TARGET: MATCHED TO NET-PITCH-ASSESSMENT.HTML
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --purple: #a855f7;

            --radius: 24px;

        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--dark);
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            color: var(--text);
            line-height: 1.8; /* Increased line height for readability */
            overflow-x: hidden;
            padding-bottom: 100px;
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 900px;
            margin: 0 auto;
            padding: 40px 20px;
        }

        /* --- TYPOGRAPHY & TRANSLATION --- */
        .vi {
            display: block;
            font-size: 0.95em;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-weight: 400;
            margin-top: 8px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            font-style: italic;
            border-left: 2px solid var(--gold);
            padding-left: 15px;
        }

        h1 {
            font-size: clamp(2.5rem, 6vw, 4.5rem);
            font-weight: 900;
            line-height: 1.1;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        h2 {
            font-size: 2.2rem;
            color: var(--gold);
            margin-bottom: 30px;
            line-height: 1.2;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-bottom: 15px;
        }

        h3 { font-size: 1.4rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 15px; margin-top: 30px; font-weight: 700; }
        
        p { margin-bottom: 15px; font-size: 1.05rem; }

        .tag { 
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; 
            color: var(--purple); 
            border: 1px solid var(--purple); 
            padding: 5px 10px; 
            border-radius: 4px; 
            font-size: 0.8rem; 
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }

        /* --- CARDS --- */
        .glass-card {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            padding: 40px; /* Increased padding */
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            margin-bottom: 60px; /* Space between sections */
        }

        /* --- SKILL BARS --- */
        .skill-row { margin-bottom: 25px; }
        .skill-label { font-weight: 700; font-size: 1rem; margin-bottom: 8px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; }
        .skill-track {
            height: 12px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            border-radius: 6px;
            overflow: hidden;
        }
        .skill-fill { height: 100%; border-radius: 6px; }
        
        .fill-crit { background: var(--danger); width: 15%; }
        .fill-low { background: var(--warning); width: 25%; }
        .fill-mid { background: var(--gold); width: 40%; }
        .fill-good { background: var(--purple); width: 65%; }

        /* --- LISTS --- */
        ul { list-style: none; margin-bottom: 20px; }
        ul li {
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            padding-left: 25px;
            position: relative;
        }
        ul li::before {
            content: '•';
            color: var(--purple);
            font-weight: bold;
            font-size: 1.5rem;
            position: absolute;
            left: 0;
            top: -5px;
        }

        /* --- OFFER BOX --- */
        .offer-box {
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.95), rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15));
            border: 2px solid var(--gold);
            text-align: center;
        }

        .price-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 20px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            text-align: left;
        }
        
        .cta-btn {
            display: block;
            width: 100%;
            background: var(--gold);
            color: #000;
            font-weight: 800;
            padding: 22px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            text-align: center;
            text-decoration: none;
            margin-top: 40px;
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            transition: transform 0.2s;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }
        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 10px 20px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="container">
    
    <header style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 80px; padding-top: 40px;">
        <span class="tag">OFFICIAL DIAGNOSTIC REPORT • CASE #8024</span>
        <h1>Full Capability Audit</h1>
        <p style="color: var(--text-dim); font-size: 1.4rem; margin-top: 15px;">
            Student: <strong>Linh</strong> | Level: <strong style="color: var(--danger);">E1 (Beginner)</strong>
        </p>
    </header>

    <section class="glass-card">
        <h2>01. Executive Summary</h2>
        <span class="vi">Tóm Tắt Đánh Giá Tổng Quan</span>
        
        <p>
            <strong>The Diagnosis:</strong> You have a significant gap between your life experience and your language ability. You are an intelligent adult operating with the English tools of a beginner. This causes frustration because you cannot express your true personality or intelligence.
        </p>
        <p>
            Currently, you are trying to translate Vietnamese directly into English. This does not work. It results in broken sentences, missing sounds, and hesitation. To fix this, we must rebuild your foundation from the ground up.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Chẩn đoán: Bạn có một khoảng cách lớn giữa vốn sống và khả năng ngôn ngữ. Bạn là người thông minh nhưng công cụ tiếng Anh lại ở mức sơ cấp. Điều này gây bức bối vì bạn không thể diễn đạt đúng con người thật của mình. Hiện tại, bạn đang cố dịch từ tiếng Việt sang tiếng Anh. Cách này không hiệu quả, dẫn đến câu bị gãy, thiếu âm và ngập ngừng. Để khắc phục, chúng ta phải xây dựng lại nền tảng từ đầu.</span>

        <div style="margin-top: 40px; padding: 20px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius: 12px;">
            <strong style="color: var(--gold);">KEY FINDING:</strong>
            <p style="margin-top: 5px;">
                You rely on "Keyword Guessing." You catch 1 or 2 words and guess the rest. You need to learn to hear and speak full structures.
            </p>
            <span class="vi" style="margin-bottom: 0;">Phát hiện chính: Bạn dựa vào việc "Đoán từ khóa". Bạn chỉ bắt được 1-2 từ và đoán phần còn lại. Bạn cần học cách nghe và nói trọn vẹn cấu trúc.</span>
        </div>
    </section>

    <section class="glass-card">
        <h2>02. Skill Audit: Listening</h2>
        <span class="vi">Đánh Giá Kỹ Năng: Nghe Hiểu</span>

        <div class="skill-row">
            <div class="skill-label"><span>Current Score</span> <span style="color:var(--danger)">2/10</span></div>
            <div class="skill-track"><div class="skill-fill fill-crit"></div></div>
        </div>

        <h3>The Problem: "Missing Sounds"</h3>
        <p>
            English is a language of ending sounds (S, K, T, Z). Vietnamese is not. Your ears are currently untrained to hear these sounds. When a native speaker talks fast, your brain deletes these sounds, making the sentence sound like a blur.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Vấn đề: "Thiếu Âm". Tiếng Anh là ngôn ngữ của âm cuối (S, K, T, Z). Tiếng Việt thì không. Tai của bạn chưa được huấn luyện để nghe những âm này. Khi người bản xứ nói nhanh, não bộ của bạn tự động xóa các âm này, khiến câu nói trở nên nhòe nhoẹt.</span>

        <h3>The Impact</h3>
        <ul>
            <li>You confuse similar words (e.g., "Fine" vs "Find" vs "Five").</li>
            <li>You cannot understand tense (Past vs. Present) because you miss the "-ed" sound.</li>
            <li>You feel panic when someone speaks at normal speed.</li>
        </ul>
        <span class="vi">Hậu quả: Bạn nhầm lẫn các từ giống nhau. Bạn không hiểu thì (Quá khứ/Hiện tại) vì bỏ lỡ âm "-ed". Bạn cảm thấy hoảng loạn khi ai đó nói tốc độ bình thường.</span>

        <h3>The Fix</h3>
        <p><strong>Immersion Training:</strong> We will not use textbooks. We will use audio drills focusing specifically on ending sounds until your ears "open up" to them. </p>
        <span class="vi">Giải pháp: Luyện nghe dồn dập. Chúng ta sẽ tập trung vào các bài tập âm cuối cho đến khi tai bạn "mở ra" để đón nhận chúng.</span>
    </section>

    <section class="glass-card">
        <h2>03. Skill Audit: Speaking</h2>
        <span class="vi">Đánh Giá Kỹ Năng: Nói & Phát Âm</span>

        <div class="skill-row">
            <div class="skill-label"><span>Current Score</span> <span style="color:var(--danger)">1/10</span></div>
            <div class="skill-track"><div class="skill-fill fill-crit"></div></div>
        </div>

        <h3>The Problem: "Broken Structure"</h3>
        <p>
            You speak in single words or broken phrases ("I go home," "She happy"). You are missing the "glue" that holds English sentences together. Specifically, you often drop the verb "To Be" (am, is, are) because Vietnamese grammar does not require it.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Vấn đề: "Gãy Cấu Trúc". Bạn nói từ đơn hoặc cụm từ gãy gọn. Bạn thiếu "keo dính" để nối các từ lại. Cụ thể, bạn thường bỏ động từ "To Be" (am, is, are) vì ngữ pháp tiếng Việt không yêu cầu.</span>

        <h3>The Physical Issue</h3>
        <p>
            Your mouth muscles are trying to speak English using Vietnamese shapes. This makes your voice soft and your pronunciation unclear. You are "swallowing" your words instead of projecting them.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Vấn đề vật lý: Cơ miệng của bạn đang cố nói tiếng Anh bằng khẩu hình tiếng Việt. Điều này làm giọng bạn nhỏ và phát âm không rõ. Bạn đang "nuốt" từ thay vì phát ra ngoài.</span>

        <h3>The Fix</h3>
        <p><strong>The 10-5-5-3 Method:</strong> We will use high-repetition drills to build new muscle memory in your mouth. You will learn to speak loudly and finish every single word. </p>
        <span class="vi">Giải pháp: Phương pháp 10-5-5-3. Chúng ta sẽ dùng bài tập lặp lại cường độ cao để xây dựng ký ức cơ bắp mới. Bạn sẽ học cách nói to và kết thúc trọn vẹn từng từ.</span>
    </section>

    <section class="glass-card">
        <h2>04. Skill Audit: Reading & Writing</h2>
        <span class="vi">Đánh Giá Kỹ Năng: Đọc & Viết</span>

        <div class="skill-row">
            <div class="skill-label"><span>Reading Score</span> <span style="color:var(--gold)">3/10</span></div>
            <div class="skill-track"><div class="skill-fill fill-mid"></div></div>
        </div>
        <div class="skill-row">
            <div class="skill-label"><span>Writing Score</span> <span style="color:var(--warning)">2/10</span></div>
            <div class="skill-track"><div class="skill-fill fill-low"></div></div>
        </div>

        <h3>The Problem: "Translation Dependency"</h3>
        <p>
            <strong>Reading:</strong> You read one word, translate it to Vietnamese in your head, then move to the next word. This is too slow. It prevents you from understanding the full meaning of a paragraph.
        </p>
        <p>
            <strong>Writing:</strong> You write English words using Vietnamese word order. (Example: "I very like it" instead of "I like it very much"). This is because you are thinking in Vietnamese first.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Vấn đề: "Phụ thuộc dịch thuật". Đọc: Bạn đọc từng từ, dịch sang tiếng Việt trong đầu, rồi mới qua từ tiếp theo. Quá chậm. Viết: Bạn dùng từ tiếng Anh nhưng sắp xếp theo trật tự tiếng Việt. Đó là do bạn đang tư duy bằng tiếng Việt trước.</span>

        <h3>The Fix</h3>
        <p><strong>Pattern Recognition:</strong> We will stop translating words and start learning "Sentence Blocks." You will learn whole phrases at once so you don't have to think about grammar rules when you write. </p>
        <span class="vi">Giải pháp: Nhận diện mẫu câu. Chúng ta sẽ ngừng dịch từng từ và bắt đầu học "Khối câu". Bạn sẽ học nguyên cụm từ để không phải nghĩ về ngữ pháp khi viết.</span>
    </section>

    <section class="glass-card">
        <h2>05. Psychometric Profile</h2>
        <span class="vi">Hồ sơ Tâm lý học (Thái độ & Tư duy)</span>

        <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 40px; margin-top: 30px;">
            <div style="text-align: center; border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px;">
                <h3 style="margin-top: 0; color: var(--purple);">MOTIVATION</h3>
                <div style="font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--success);">High</div>
                <p style="font-size: 0.9rem;">You are eager to change. You understand the value of English and are willing to invest time.</p>
                <span class="vi">Động lực Cao. Bạn khao khát thay đổi. Bạn hiểu giá trị của tiếng Anh và sẵn sàng đầu tư thời gian.</span>
            </div>

            <div style="text-align: center; border: 1px solid var(--border); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px;">
                <h3 style="margin-top: 0; color: var(--gold);">CONFIDENCE</h3>
                <div style="font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--danger);">Low</div>
                <p style="font-size: 0.9rem;">You are paralyzed by the fear of being wrong. You prefer silence over making a mistake.</p>
                <span class="vi">Tự tin Thấp. Bạn bị tê liệt bởi nỗi sợ sai. Bạn thà im lặng còn hơn là nói sai.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <h3 style="margin-top: 40px;">The Strategy</h3>
        <p>
            We cannot build skills without building confidence first. Our classes are designed as a "Safe Space." We celebrate mistakes because mistakes mean you are trying. We will force you to be loud until it feels natural.
        </p>
        <span class="vi">Chiến lược: Chúng ta không thể xây dựng kỹ năng nếu không xây dựng sự tự tin trước. Lớp học là "Vùng An Toàn". Chúng tôi ăn mừng lỗi sai vì sai nghĩa là bạn đang cố gắng. Chúng tôi sẽ ép bạn nói to cho đến khi nó trở thành tự nhiên.</span>
    </section>

    <section class="glass-card offer-box">
        <h2 style="color: var(--gold); border-bottom: none; margin-bottom: 10px;">06. The 90-Day Protocol</h2>
        <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin-bottom: 40px;">Quy trình Huấn luyện 90 ngày</span>
        
        <p style="margin-bottom: 40px; text-align: center;">
            To fix these foundational issues, we need intensity. I recommend a <strong>Hybrid Schedule</strong> that combines the energy of a group with the precision of private mentoring.
            <br><span class="vi" style="border:none;">Để sửa chữa nền tảng này, chúng ta cần cường độ cao. Tôi đề xuất Lịch học Kết hợp: Năng lượng nhóm + Sự chính xác cá nhân.</span>
        </p>

        <div style="text-align: left; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); padding: 30px; border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            
            <div class="price-row">
                <div>
                    <strong style="color: #fff; font-size: 1.2rem;">1. Group Immersion (BlueTech Class)</strong>
                    <div style="font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-dim); margin-top: 5px;">
                        • Tuesday 18:00 - 19:00 (In-Person)<br>
                        • Saturday 14:30 - 16:00 (Online)
                    </div>
                    <div class="vi" style="margin-bottom: 0; border: none; padding: 0; margin-top: 5px;">Lớp nhóm: Tương tác và phản xạ nhanh.</div>
                </div>
                <div style="text-align: right;">
                    <div style="font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; color: var(--purple); font-weight: 700; font-size: 1.2rem;">750,000 ₫</div>

            <div class="price-row">

                        • 1 Hour Per Week (Flexible Schedule)
                    </div>
                    <div class="vi" style="margin-bottom: 0; border: none; padding: 0; margin-top: 5px;">Lớp 1-1: Chỉnh sửa phát âm chi tiết.</div>

            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; padding-top: 30px; margin-top: 10px;">

    <style>

        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

           GOSPEL 444: INTERACTIVE DASHBOARD

           UX: TABBED NAVIGATION | LANGUAGE: SIMPLE E1

        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */

        :root {

            --purple: #a855f7;

            --gold: #f59e0b;

            --dark: #0f0f1a;

            --panel: #1a1a2e;

            --text: #ffffff;

            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.6);

            --danger: #ef4444;

            --warning: #f97316;

            --success: #10b981;

        }



        * { margin: 0; padding: 0; box-sizing: border-box; }



        body {

            background-color: var(--dark);

            color: var(--text);

            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;

            line-height: 1.5;

            display: flex;

            justify-content: center;

            min-height: 100vh;

            padding: 20px;

        }



        .dashboard-container {

            width: 100%;

            max-width: 900px;

            background: rgba(20, 20, 35, 0.8);

            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);

            border-radius: 24px;

        }



        /* --- HEADER --- */

        header {

            padding: 30px;

            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);

            text-align: center;

        }

        .status-badge {

            display: inline-block;

            background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15);

            color: var(--danger);

            padding: 5px 12px;

            border-radius: 50px;

            font-size: 0.8rem;

            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';

            border: 1px solid var(--danger);

        }



        /* --- TABS --- */

        .tabs {

            display: flex;

            overflow-x: auto;

            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);

            padding: 10px;

            gap: 10px;

            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);

        }

        

        .tab-btn {

            background: transparent;

            border: none;

            color: var(--text-dim);

            padding: 12px 20px;

            border-radius: 8px;

            cursor: pointer;

            font-weight: 600;

            font-family: 'Inter';

            font-size: 0.9rem;

            white-space: nowrap;

            transition: 0.2s;

        }

        

        .tab-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }

        

        .tab-btn.active {

            background: var(--purple);

            color: #fff;

            box-shadow: 0 4px 15px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);

        }



        /* --- CONTENT AREA --- */

        .content-area {

            padding: 40px;

            min-height: 400px;

        }



        .tab-content { display: none; animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease; }

        .tab-content.active { display: block; }



        @keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }



        /* --- METRICS & VISUALS --- */

        .score-big { font-size: 4rem; font-weight: 900; line-height: 1; margin-bottom: 10px; }

        .score-red { color: var(--danger); }

        .score-orange { color: var(--warning); }

        .vi {

            color: var(--text-dim);

            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);

        }



        /* --- OFFER --- */

        .price-tag { font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--gold); text-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }

        .cta-btn {

            transition: 0.2s;

        }

        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 10px 30px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }



    </style>

</head>

<body>



<div class="dashboard-container">

    

    <header>

        <div style="font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--purple); margin-bottom: 5px;">NET STUDENT DASHBOARD</div>

        <h1>LINH'S PROFILE</h1>

        <span class="status-badge">LEVEL: E1 (BEGINNER)</span>

    </header>



    <div class="tabs">

        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('plan')">💎 The Plan</button>

    </div>



    <div class="content-area">

        

        <div id="summary" class="tab-content active">

            <h2>Executive Summary</h2>

            <span class="vi">Tóm tắt tình trạng</span>

            <p class="box">

                You have a "Smart Mind" but "Empty Hands." You have life experience, but you lack the English tools to express it.

                <br><span class="vi" style="border:none; padding:0; margin:0;">Bạn có tư duy tốt nhưng thiếu công cụ. Bạn không thể diễn đạt con người thật của mình bằng tiếng Anh.</span>

            </p>

            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px;">

                <div class="box" style="text-align: center;">

                    <div class="score-big score-red">15%</div>

                    <div>Current Skill</div>

                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; text-align: center;">Kỹ năng hiện tại</span>

                </div>

                <div class="box" style="text-align: center;">

                    <div class="score-big score-gold">90%</div>

                    <div>Potential</div>

                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; text-align: center;">Tiềm năng phát triển</span>

                </div>

            </div>

        </div>



        <div id="listening" class="tab-content">

            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">

                <h2>Listening (Nghe)</h2>

                <div class="score-big score-red" style="font-size: 2.5rem;">2/10</div>

            </div>

            

            <div class="box">

                <strong>Problem: The "Gap"</strong>

                <p>You only hear 1 or 2 words in a sentence. You guess the rest. If you guess wrong, you panic.</p>

                <span class="vi">Bạn chỉ nghe được 1-2 từ khóa và đoán phần còn lại. Nếu đoán sai, bạn sẽ hoảng loạn.</span>

            </div>



            <div class="box">

                <strong>Why? (Nguyên nhân)</strong>

                <p>English has ending sounds (s, t, k). Vietnamese does not. Your ears are deleting these sounds.</p>

                <span class="vi">Tai của bạn đang tự động xóa bỏ các âm cuối của tiếng Anh (s, t, k) vì tiếng Việt không có chúng.</span>

            </div>

        </div>



        <div id="speaking" class="tab-content">

            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">

                <h2>Speaking (Nói)</h2>

                <div class="score-big score-red" style="font-size: 2.5rem;">1/10</div>

            </div>



            <div class="box">

                <strong>Problem: No Sentence Structure</strong>

                <p>You speak in single words. "Me... go... home." You do not connect them.</p>

                <span class="vi">Bạn chỉ nói từ đơn lẻ. Không kết nối thành câu hoàn chỉnh.</span>

            </div>



            <div class="box">

                <strong>Critical Error: The "To Be" Verb</strong>

                <p>You say: "She happy." (Vietnamese logic)<br>Correct: "She <strong>IS</strong> happy." (English logic)</p>

                <span class="vi">Lỗi thiếu động từ "To Be". Bạn đang dùng logic tiếng Việt để nói tiếng Anh.</span>

            </div>

        </div>



        <div id="readwrite" class="tab-content">

            <div style="display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px;">

                <div>

                    <h3>Reading (Đọc)</h3>

                    <div class="score-big score-orange" style="font-size: 2rem;">3/10</div>

                    <p style="font-size: 0.9rem;">You can understand signs and menus. You rely too much on Google Translate.</p>

                    <span class="vi">Hiểu biển báo/menu. Phụ thuộc quá nhiều vào Google Dịch.</span>

                </div>

                <div>

                    <h3>Writing (Viết)</h3>

                    <div class="score-big score-orange" style="font-size: 2rem;">2/10</div>

                    <p style="font-size: 0.9rem;">You write English words using Vietnamese grammar order.</p>

                    <span class="vi">Viết từ tiếng Anh nhưng theo trật tự ngữ pháp tiếng Việt.</span>

                </div>

            </div>

        </div>



        <div id="mindset" class="tab-content">

            <h2>Psychology (Tâm Lý)</h2>

            

            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--success);">

                <strong>Motivation: HIGH (6.5/10)</strong>

                <p>You want to change. You are ready to work.</p>

                <span class="vi">Động lực cao. Bạn đã sẵn sàng để thay đổi.</span>

            </div>



            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--danger);">

                <strong>Confidence: LOW (1.5/10)</strong>

                <p>You are afraid to be wrong. You whisper because you don't want people to hear mistakes.</p>

                <span class="vi">Bạn sợ sai. Bạn nói lí nhí vì sợ người khác nghe thấy lỗi của mình.</span>

            </div>

        </div>



        <div id="plan" class="tab-content">

            <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">

                <h2 style="color: var(--gold);">The Solution</h2>

                <p>A Hybrid Schedule: Group Energy + Private Focus</p>

                <span class="vi" style="border:none; padding:0; margin:0;">Lịch học kết hợp: Năng lượng nhóm + Tập trung cá nhân</span>

            </div>



            <div class="box">

                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">

                    <strong>1. BlueTech Group Class</strong>

                    <span style="color: var(--purple); font-weight: 700;">750,000 ₫</span>

                </div>

                <p style="font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-dim);">

                    • TUE 18:00 (In-Person)<br>

                    • SAT 14:30 (Online)

                </p>

            </div>



            <div class="box">

                <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">

                    <strong>2. Private Mentoring</strong>

                    <span style="color: var(--purple); font-weight: 700;">600,000 ₫</span>

                </div>

                <p style="font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--text-dim);">

                    • 1 Hour / Week (Flexible)<br>

                    • Fix pronunciation & grammar gaps.

                </p>

            </div>



            <div style="text-align: center; margin-top: 30px;">

                <div style="font-size: 0.9rem; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px;">Total Weekly Investment</div>

                <div class="price-tag">1,350,000 ₫</div>

                <a href="https://zalo.me" class="cta-btn">START NOW</a>

                <p style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-dim); margin-top: 10px;">*Results Guaranteed in 90 Days</p>

            </div>

        </div>



    </div>



</div>



<script>

    function openTab(tabName) {

        // Hide all tabs

        var contents = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-content");

        for (var i = 0; i < contents.length; i++) {

            contents[i].classList.remove("active");

        }

        

        // Deactivate all buttons

        var btns = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-btn");

        for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {

            btns[i].classList.remove("active");

        }



        // Show selected tab

        document.getElementById(tabName).classList.add("active");

        

        // Activate button

        event.currentTarget.classList.add("active");

    }

</script>



</body>

</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET Dashboard: Linh</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444: INTERACTIVE DASHBOARD (EXPANDED EDITION)
           VISUALS: MATCHED TO NET-PITCH-ASSESSMENT
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --purple: #a855f7;
            --gold: #f59e0b;
            --dark: #0f0f1a;
            --glass: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.9);
            --border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --text: #ffffff;
            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
            --radius: 24px;
            --danger: #ef4444;
            --warning: #f97316;
            --success: #10b981;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--dark);
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            color: var(--text);
            line-height: 1.6;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            padding: 20px;
        }

        .dashboard-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 1000px;
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 20px 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        header {
            padding: 40px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1), transparent);
        }
        
        h1 {
            font-size: clamp(2rem, 5vw, 3rem);
            font-weight: 900;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .tag {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--gold);
            border: 1px solid var(--gold);
            padding: 5px 10px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }

        .vi-header {
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-style: italic;
            font-size: 1rem;
        }

        /* --- TABS NAVIGATION --- */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            overflow-x: auto;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            padding: 15px;
            gap: 10px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        
        .tab-btn {
            background: transparent;
            border: 1px solid transparent;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 600;
            font-family: 'Inter';
            font-size: 1rem;
            white-space: nowrap;
            transition: 0.3s;
        }
        
        .tab-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }
        
        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            border-color: var(--purple);
        }

        /* --- CONTENT AREA --- */
        .content-area {
            padding: 40px;
            min-height: 500px;
        }

        .tab-content { display: none; animation: fadeIn 0.4s ease; }
        .tab-content.active { display: block; }

        @keyframes fadeIn { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- TYPOGRAPHY INSIDE TABS --- */
        h2 { font-size: 2rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: 800; }
        h3 { font-size: 1.3rem; color: var(--gold); margin-top: 30px; margin-bottom: 15px; font-weight: 700; }
        
        .vi {
            display: block;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-style: italic;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            border-left: 3px solid var(--gold);
            padding-left: 15px;
            margin-top: 5px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
        }

        /* --- COMPONENTS --- */
        .box {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            padding: 25px;
            border-radius: 16px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }

        .alert-box {
            background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);
            border: 1px solid var(--danger);
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
        }

        .success-box {
            background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1);
            border: 1px solid var(--success);
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
        }

        /* --- METRICS --- */
        .score-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 20px; margin-bottom: 30px; }
        .score-card { text-align: center; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; }
        
        .big-num { font-size: 3.5rem; font-weight: 900; line-height: 1; margin-bottom: 5px; }
        .text-red { color: var(--danger); }
        .text-orange { color: var(--warning); }
        .text-gold { color: var(--gold); }
        .text-green { color: var(--success); }

        /* --- PRICING --- */
        .price-row {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            padding: 20px 0;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
        }
        
        .total-price { font-size: 3rem; font-weight: 900; color: var(--gold); text-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.3); }
        
        .cta-btn {
            display: block; width: 100%; text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--gold), #fbbf24);
            color: #000;
            padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px;
            font-weight: 800; text-decoration: none; margin-top: 30px;
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
            text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: 1px;
        }
        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 10px 40px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.4); }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="dashboard-container">
    
    <header>
        <span class="tag">NET STUDENT DASHBOARD</span>
        <h1>LINH'S PROFILE</h1>
        <div class="vi-header">Status: E1 (Beginner) | Archetype: The Sleeping Tiger</div>
    </header>

    <div class="tabs">
        <button class="tab-btn active" onclick="openTab('summary')">🏠 Overview</button>
        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('listening')">👂 Listening</button>
        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('speaking')">🗣️ Speaking</button>
        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('readwrite')">📖 Read/Write</button>
        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('mindset')">🧠 Mindset</button>
        <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('plan')">💎 The Plan</button>
    </div>

    <div class="content-area">
        
        <div id="summary" class="tab-content active">
            <h2>Executive Summary</h2>
            <span class="vi">Tóm tắt tình trạng</span>
            
            <div class="box">
                <p style="font-size: 1.1rem; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    <strong>The Verdict:</strong> You have a "Smart Mind" but "Empty Hands." You have clear thoughts and life experience, but you lack the basic English tools to express them.
                </p>
                <span class="vi">Kết luận: Bạn có tư duy tốt nhưng thiếu công cụ. Bạn có suy nghĩ rõ ràng và vốn sống, nhưng thiếu công cụ tiếng Anh cơ bản để diễn đạt chúng.</span>
                
                <p style="font-size: 1.1rem; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                    Currently, you are trying to translate Vietnamese directly into English. This does not work. It creates broken sentences and fear.
                </p>
                <span class="vi">Hiện tại, bạn đang cố dịch trực tiếp từ tiếng Việt sang tiếng Anh. Cách này không hiệu quả. Nó tạo ra những câu gãy gọn và nỗi sợ hãi.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="score-grid">
                <div class="score-card">
                    <div class="big-num text-red">15%</div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 700;">Current Ability</div>
                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; font-size:0.8rem;">Khả năng hiện tại</span>
                </div>
                <div class="score-card">
                    <div class="big-num text-gold">90%</div>
                    <div style="font-weight: 700;">Growth Potential</div>
                    <span class="vi" style="border:none; margin:0; font-size:0.8rem;">Tiềm năng phát triển</span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="listening" class="tab-content">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                <h2>Listening Audit</h2>
                <div class="big-num text-red">2/10</div>
            </div>
            <span class="vi">Đánh giá kỹ năng Nghe</span>
            
            <div class="alert-box">
                <strong>The Problem: "The Gap"</strong>
                <p style="margin-top: 5px;">
                    You only hear 1 or 2 keywords in a sentence. You guess the rest. If you guess wrong, you panic.
                </p>
                <span class="vi" style="border-color: var(--danger);">Vấn đề: Bạn chỉ nghe được 1-2 từ khóa và đoán phần còn lại. Nếu đoán sai, bạn sẽ hoảng loạn.</span>
            </div>

            <h3>Why is this happening?</h3>
            <div class="box">
                <p><strong>1. Missing Sounds:</strong> English has ending sounds (s, t, k). Vietnamese does not. Your ears are literally "deleting" these sounds because they seem like noise.</p>
                <span class="vi">Thiếu âm: Tiếng Anh có âm cuối. Tiếng Việt thì không. Tai của bạn đang "xóa" những âm này vì tưởng là tiếng ồn.</span>
                
                <p><strong>2. Speed Panic:</strong> When people speak fast, your brain freezes. You are trying to translate word-by-word, but they speak too fast for that.</p>
                <span class="vi">Hoảng loạn tốc độ: Khi người khác nói nhanh, não bạn đóng băng. Bạn đang cố dịch từng từ, nhưng họ nói quá nhanh để làm điều đó.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="success-box">
                <strong>The Fix:</strong> Immersion. No textbooks. We listen to real audio until your ears "open up" to the new sounds.
                <br><span class="vi" style="border-color: var(--success);">Giải pháp: Tắm ngôn ngữ. Không giáo trình. Nghe âm thanh thực tế cho đến khi tai bạn "mở ra".</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="speaking" class="tab-content">
            <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;">
                <h2>Speaking Audit</h2>
                <div class="big-num text-red">1/10</div>
            </div>
            <span class="vi">Đánh giá kỹ năng Nói</span>

            <div class="alert-box">
                <strong>The Problem: "Broken Structure"</strong>
                <p style="margin-top: 5px;">
                    You speak in single words. "Me... go... home." You do not connect them into a flow.
                </p>
                <span class="vi" style="border-color: var(--danger);">Vấn đề: Bạn nói từ đơn lẻ. Không kết nối thành dòng chảy.</span>
            </div>

            <h3>Critical Errors Identified</h3>
            <div class="box">
                <p><strong>1. The "To Be" Error:</strong> You say "She happy." (Vietnamese logic). In English, you must say "She IS happy." You are missing the glue words.</p>
                <span class="vi">Lỗi động từ "To Be": Bạn nói theo logic tiếng Việt (Cô ấy vui). Tiếng Anh cần động từ nối (Cô ấy LÀ vui).</span>

                <p><strong>2. The Whisper:</strong> You speak very softly. This is because you are afraid of being wrong. But in English, volume = confidence.</p>
                <span class="vi">Nói thầm: Bạn nói rất nhỏ vì sợ sai. Nhưng trong tiếng Anh, âm lượng = sự tự tin.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="success-box">
                <strong>The Fix:</strong> Muscle Memory Training. We will drill sentences repetitively until your mouth moves automatically, without thinking.
                <br><span class="vi" style="border-color: var(--success);">Giải pháp: Luyện ký ức cơ bắp. Lặp lại câu cho đến khi miệng tự động nói mà không cần suy nghĩ.</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="readwrite" class="tab-content">
            <h2>Literacy Audit</h2>
            <span class="vi">Đánh giá Đọc & Viết</span>

            <div class="score-grid">
                <div class="score-card">
                    <div class="big-num text-orange">3/10</div>
                    <strong>Reading</strong>
                </div>
                <div class="score-card">
                    <div class="big-num text-orange">2/10</div>
                    <strong>Writing</strong>
                </div>
            </div>

            <h3>The Analysis</h3>
            <div class="box">
                <p><strong>Reading:</strong> You can understand signs, menus, and simple messages. But you rely heavily on Google Translate for anything longer than one sentence.</p>
                <span class="vi">Đọc: Hiểu biển báo, menu. Nhưng phụ thuộc quá nhiều vào Google Dịch cho câu dài.</span>

                <p><strong>Writing:</strong> You write English words using Vietnamese grammar order. Example: "I very like it" (Incorrect) vs "I like it very much" (Correct).</p>
                <span class="vi">Viết: Bạn dùng từ tiếng Anh nhưng sắp xếp theo trật tự ngữ pháp tiếng Việt.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="success-box">
                <strong>The Fix:</strong> Pattern Recognition. Stop learning words. Start learning "Chunks" (groups of words that always go together).
                <br><span class="vi" style="border-color: var(--success);">Giải pháp: Nhận diện mẫu câu. Ngừng học từ đơn. Bắt đầu học "Cụm từ".</span>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="mindset" class="tab-content">
            <h2>Psychology Profile</h2>
            <span class="vi">Hồ sơ Tâm lý học</span>
            
            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--success);">
                <h3 style="margin-top: 0; color: var(--success);">MOTIVATION: HIGH (6.5/10)</h3>
                <p>You want to change. You know English is the key to your future. You are ready to work hard.</p>
                <span class="vi" style="border:none; padding:0;">Động lực cao. Bạn muốn thay đổi. Bạn biết tiếng Anh là chìa khóa và sẵn sàng làm việc chăm chỉ.</span>
            </div>

            <div class="box" style="border-left: 4px solid var(--danger);">
                <h3 style="margin-top: 0; color: var(--danger);">CONFIDENCE: LOW (1.5/10)</h3>
