<script>
    function openTab(tabName) {
        // Hide all tabs
        var contents = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-content");
        for (var i = 0; i < contents.length; i++) {
            contents[i].classList.remove("active");
        }
        
        // Deactivate all buttons
        var btns = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-btn");
        for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {
            btns[i].classList.remove("active");
        }

        // Show selected tab
        document.getElementById(tabName).classList.add("active");
        
        // Activate button
        event.currentTarget.classList.add("active");
    }
</script>

    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444: THE ULTIMATE DASHBOARD
           FEATURES: FEATURE-RICH | E1 LANGUAGE | HIGH IMPACT
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --purple: #a855f7;
            --gold: #f59e0b;
            --dark: #0f0f1a;
            --glass: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.95);
            --border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --text: #ffffff;
            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
            --radius: 24px;
            --danger: #ef4444;
            --warning: #f97316;
            --success: #10b981;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--dark);
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            color: var(--text);
            line-height: 1.6;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            padding: 20px;
        }

        .dashboard-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 1000px;
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 30px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        header {
            padding: 35px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15), transparent);
        }
        
        h1 {
            font-size: clamp(2rem, 5vw, 3.5rem);
            font-weight: 900;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .tag {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--gold);
            border: 1px solid var(--gold);
            padding: 6px 12px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            font-weight: 700;
        }

        /* --- TABS NAVIGATION --- */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            overflow-x: auto;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            padding: 15px;
            gap: 10px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        
        .tab-btn {
            background: transparent;
            border: 1px solid transparent;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 700;
            font-family: 'Inter';
            font-size: 1rem;
            white-space: nowrap;
            transition: 0.3s;
        }
        
        .tab-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }
        
        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5);
            border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
        }

        /* --- CONTENT AREA --- */
        .content-area { padding: 40px; min-height: 500px; }
        .tab-content { display: none; animation: slideUp 0.4s ease; }
        .tab-content.active { display: block; }

        @keyframes slideUp { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- TYPOGRAPHY INSIDE TABS --- */
        h2 { font-size: 2.2rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: 900; }
        h3 { font-size: 1.4rem; color: var(--gold); margin-top: 35px; margin-bottom: 15px; font-weight: 700; display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; }
        
        .vi {
            display: block;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            font-style: italic;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            border-left: 3px solid var(--gold);
            padding-left: 15px;
            margin-top: 8px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
        }

        /* --- COMPONENTS --- */
        .box {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.03);
            padding: 30px;
            border-radius: 16px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);

        .alert-box {
            background: rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.1);
            border: 1px solid var(--danger);
            padding: 25px;

        /* --- METRICS --- */

            transition: transform 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
            text-transform: uppercase;
        }
        .cta-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-3px); box-shadow: 0 15px 50px rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5); }

    </style>
</head>

        :root {

            --border: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            --text: #ffffff;
            --text-dim: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.7);
            --radius: 24px;
            --danger: #ef4444;
            --warning: #f97316;
            --success: #10b981;
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--dark);
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 0% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 50%),
                radial-gradient(circle at 100% 100%, rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1) 0%, transparent 50%);
            background-attachment: fixed;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            color: var(--text);
            line-height: 1.6;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            padding: 20px;
        }

        .dashboard-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 1000px;
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
            border-radius: var(--radius);
            backdrop-filter: blur(12px);
            overflow: hidden;
            box-shadow: 0 30px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        header {
            padding: 35px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15), transparent);
        }
        
        h1 {
            font-size: clamp(2rem, 5vw, 3.5rem);
            font-weight: 900;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
        }

        .tag {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--gold);
            border: 1px solid var(--gold);
            padding: 6px 12px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            font-weight: 700;
        }

        /* --- TABS NAVIGATION --- */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            overflow-x: auto;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3);
            padding: 15px;
            gap: 10px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
        }
        
        .tab-btn {
            background: transparent;
            border: 1px solid transparent;
            color: var(--text-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-weight: 700;
            font-family: 'Inter';
            font-size: 1rem;
            white-space: nowrap;
            transition: 0.3s;
        }
        
        .tab-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }
        
        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 25px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5);
            border-color: rgba(255,255,255,0.2);
        }

        /* --- CONTENT AREA --- */
        .content-area { padding: 40px; min-height: 500px; }
        .tab-content { display: none; animation: slideUp 0.4s ease; }
        .tab-content.active { display: block; }

        @keyframes slideUp { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- TYPOGRAPHY INSIDE TABS --- */
        h2 { font-size: 2.2rem; color: #fff; margin-bottom: 5px; font-weight: 900; }

"teacher ‘disappointed’ for fun",

"beer redemption arc for Dec 5"

],

focus: ["pronunciation (th/s)", "storytelling simple", "weekend highlights"]

}

}

]

},



E3_I1: {

description:

"Pre-intermediate / intermediate professionals at BlueTech; focus on sarcasm, context, tone, business English.",

exemplarStudents: [

{

id: "e3i1_linh",

level: "I1",

role: "Key Account Executive – Application Specialist",

hometown: "Ca Mau",

focus: ["sarcasm", "tone", "negotiation phrasing", "hometown description"]

},

{

id: "e3i1_huong",

level: "E3",

role: "Project Executive",

lore: ["flying boat vs roller coaster"],

focus: ["clarifying questions", "future tense", "business idioms"]

},

{

id: "e3i1_tien",

level: "E3",

focus: [

"storytelling structure",

"expressing emotions",

"soft ‘H’ pronunciation"

],

"communication workshop about personality + self-development",

"moving house logistics",

"sandal-based husband sarcasm"

]

},

{

}

]

}

},



// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// 3. MODES & BEHAVIOUR (TRANSLATE MODE, LEVEL MODE)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

behavior_modes: {

translate_mode_vi_saigon_informal: {

description:

"When activated, all English teacher lines were translated to casual Saigon-area Vietnamese.",

rules: [

"No extra explanation.",

"No options.",

"No grammar teaching.",

"Keep tone natural, slightly playful, not too formal.",

"Mirror teacher energy (e.g., ‘Quyyyy ơiiii’, ‘Denny ơiiiiiii’)."

],

usageExamples: [

{

en: "Why do you stay home on the weekend and no go out for a wild night of dancing on tables with Linh?",

vi: "Sao cuối tuần bạn lại ở nhà, không ra ngoài đi quậy, nhảy trên bàn với Linh?"

},

{

en: "What the heck is a 5D film!?",

vi: "Phim 5D là cái quái gì vậy!?"

},

{

en: "Are you feeling better since I last spoke with you, Quynh?",

vi: "Bạn thấy đỡ hơn từ lần cuối tôi nói chuyện với bạn không, Quỳnh?"

}

]

},



level_handling: {

levels: ["E1", "E2", "E3", "I1"],

notes: {

E1:

"Ultra-simple sentences, 1 clause, minimal tense variety, heavy teacher modeling.",

E2:

"Slightly longer answers, basic patterns like ‘My favorite thing was… because…’.",

E3:

"Can handle simple paragraphs, describing hometowns, giving reasons with because.",

I1:

"Works with sarcasm, irony, context vs literal meaning, short debates, idioms."

}

}

},



// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// 4. LESSON CONTENT – THEMES & SCENES

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

lessonThemes: {

sarcasm_and_tone: {

definitions: {

context:

"The situation and background that gives words their meaning. Without context, you can’t tell if someone is joking or serious.",

tone:

"The emotion or attitude in someone’s voice or writing. A sarcastic tone can show irritation or annoyance.",

literal:

"The exact, real meaning of the words (not figurative). ‘Don’t take it literally—she was being sarcastic!’",

irony:

"When reality is opposite to what is expected or said. Example: the safety instructor gets injured.",

exaggerate:

"To make something sound bigger, better, or worse than it really is."

},

workplaceWhy: [

"Understanding context in meetings and emails.",

"Detecting tone to respond correctly to feedback.",

"Distinguishing literal vs ironic meaning to avoid misunderstandings.",

"Strong communication (including sarcasm awareness) makes you a better team member.",

"Taking responsibility and showing initiative supports career growth.",

"Meeting deadlines and collaborating effectively is a universal workplace need."

],

studentSarcasmExercise: {

description:

"Teacher goes around the room, asks each student to produce sarcasm in a safe, simple scenario.",

examples: [

{

student: "Huong",

prompt: "Stuck in traffic",

raw: "wooow! uhhh perfect day, the road is so smoothie",

normalized:

"Wow, perfect day! The traffic is sooo smooth today.",

explanation: "Words positive, reality negative → sarcasm."

},

{

student: "Tien",

prompt:

"Husband returning drunk at 1AM, third time this week.",

raw:

"You go home soon, you can go continue, go drink more bia. *hits him with a sandal*",

normalized:

"Wow, you’re home so early! Go ahead, go drink more bia!",

note:

"Great sarcasm in Vietnamese context; discussion that this is not workplace-appropriate but useful for tone training."

},

{

teacherSuggestionForQuy: {

context:

"Super busy with deliveries; someone asks, ‘Are you busy?’",

sarcasticLine:

"Oh yes, I have so much free time today, we should just go on vacation!"

}

}

]

},

workplaceSarcasmPrompt: {

prompt:

"Your colleague forgot an important deadline for the third time. Write a sarcastic comment using words like responsibility, professional, or impressed.",

modelAnswers: [

"Wow, your sense of responsibility is amazing. You’re really a professional… at missing deadlines.",

"Wow, so professional. I’m really impressed you missed the deadline again."

]

}

},



weekend_highlights_and_beer_lore: {

denny_arc: {

reportedBeers: 6,

teacherReaction: "mock_disappointment",

redemptionPlan: {

date: "2025-12-05",

time: "18:00",

detail:

"Networking dinner; Denny promises to ‘make up for his failure’ by drinking 12 bia.",

teacherJokes: [

"Denny, 6 bia is unacceptable.",

"Teacher Michael is disappointed.",

"We need to fix your 6-bia mistake on December 5th."

]

}

},

weekendPatterns: {

template: "My favorite thing about the weekend was ___ because ___.",

examples: [

{

who: "Huong",

theme: "Roller coaster",

description:

"It was exciting and scary, she screamed a lot but in the end it was very fun."

},

{

who: "Quy",

theme: "5D film",

description:

"She saw many countries in the world; it was ‘so amazing!’"

},

{

who: "Denny",

theme: "Nature",

description:

"Liked seeing animals and walking along / beside the ocean; very peaceful; also ate seafood."

},

{

who: "Huy (example)",

theme: "Animals",

line: "My favorite thing about the weekend was seeing all the animals."

}

]

}

},



population_and_countries: {

canadaSizeSegment: {

teacherHometown: "Calgary",

province: "Alberta",

script: [

"My hometown is called Calgary.",

"It is located in the province Alberta.",

"It is on the west side of Canada.",

"Alberta is bigger than all of Vietnam in area, but only has about 4.5 million people."

],

teachingPoints: [

"West vs East of Canada.",

"Using landmarks that foreigners know: Vancouver, Toronto.",

"Teacher sometimes says ‘near Vancouver’ even though it’s 12 hours by car, to help foreigners understand."

]

},

top10CountriesByArea: {

simpleList: [

"Russia",

"Canada",

"China",

"United States (USA)",

"Brazil",

"Australia",

"India",

"Argentina",

"Kazakhstan",

"Algeria"

],

teachingNotes: {

india:

"India is very big but is 7th by area; used to practice ‘big but not top 4’ reasoning.",

comparisonLanguage:

"Used E2 sentences: ‘The largest country in the world is Russia. Canada is the second largest. China is the third. The fourth is the USA.’"

}

}

},



directions_and_hometowns: {

directionalLanguage: {

compass: ["North", "West", "East", "South"],

diagonals: ["NW", "NE", "SW", "SE"],

vi_labels: {

N: "Bắc",

W: "Tây",

E: "Đông",

S: "Nam",

NW: "Tây Bắc",

NE: "Đông Bắc",

SW: "Tây Nam",

SE: "Đông Nam"

}

},

saigon_to_phan_thiet: {

E2Explanation:

"From Saigon, you drive east on the highway for about 3–4 hours. You pass Đồng Nai, go toward Bình Thuận, and then you arrive in Phan Thiết near the beach and Mũi Né.",

targets: [

"Use ‘east/east side’ language.",

"Practice highway, hour, arrive, beach."

]

},

ca_mau_hometown_script: {

E3Model:

"My hometown is Cà Mau. It is in the very south of Vietnam, at the bottom of the country. From Saigon, you travel south for about 7–8 hours by bus. Cà Mau city has around 300,000 people, and the whole province has about 1.2 million. A famous landmark is Mũi Cà Mau, the southernmost point of Vietnam. The vibe is peaceful and friendly. Life is slower than Saigon, and you can see many rivers, forests, and a lot of seafood.",

teachingFocus: [

"Location using north/south.",

"Population (round numbers).",

"Vibe adjectives: peaceful, friendly, slower.",

"Landmarks as anchors."

]

}

},



cultural_notes_breakfast_bacon_cheese: {

teacherComments: [

"Very weird for a foreigner first coming to Vietnam and see the breakfast you guys eat.",

"In Vietnam you eat dinner for breakfast, it's very strange.",

"When I go visit Canada, I eat like 5kg of bacon in 5 days, super unhealthy and delicious.",

"There is no bacon and no real cheese in Vietnam, sometimes it makes me sad."

],

usage:

"Used for light cultural discussion with E1/E2; also to practice ‘healthy/unhealthy’ adjectives and simple feelings (happy/sad)."

}

},



// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// 5. JOLLY PHONICS / PRONUNCIATION CONTENT

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

phonics_and_pronunciation: {

groups: {

group1: ["s", "a", "t", "i", "p", "n"],

group2: ["c", "k", "e", "h", "r", "m", "d"],

group3: ["g", "o", "u", "l", "f", "b"]

},

E1_paragraphs: [

{

group: 1,

text:

"Sam and Tina sit at a small table in a little shop near the park. Sam has a tin of snacks, and Tina has a big map. They talk about a trip they want to take on Saturday. Sam taps on the map and smiles because he can see a nice path to the sea. Tina nods and starts to plan the time and the places they will visit. It is a simple plan, but they are excited."

},

{

group: 2,

text:

"Kim has a red hat and a small orange backpack. She gets up early in the morning and checks the weather. The air is cool, and the road is quiet. Kim walks to her friend’s home to meet her for a short ride to the market. She sees her friend on the street and waves. They talk, laugh, and head down the road together. It is a calm and happy start to the day."

},

{

group: 3,

text:

"A big brown dog lives on a farm near a long green field. Every morning the dog runs around the farm and looks for something fun to do. He likes to play with a blue ball and roll on the soft grass. The dog often follows the farmer to the barn and watches him work. The farm is quiet, but the dog makes the place feel full of life and energy."

}

],

E3_paragraphs: [

{

group: 1,

text:

"Late in the afternoon, Nina and Tim sit in a small city café with a large map spread out on the table. They want to plan a trip for the weekend, so they take notes, circle places, and discuss ideas. Tim suggests visiting a small island nearby because the weather will be nice, and Nina agrees. She adds that they should start early in the morning to avoid traffic. They sit for almost an hour, talking, laughing, and imagining how relaxing the weekend will be."

},

{

group: 2,

text:

"Mark checks his red helmet and makes sure the strap is tight before he gets on his bike. He enjoys riding on the road early in the day when the air is fresh and the streets are not crowded. On the way to work, he sometimes stops at a small market to buy warm bread and fresh fruit. He likes the rhythm of the morning: the quiet sound of the road, the smell of breakfast, and the friendly wave of the shop owner as he rides by."

},

{

group: 3,

text:

"On a quiet farm near a long forest line, a little dog spends his day running through the open fields. He follows the farmer from the house to the barn and watches him feed the animals. In the afternoon, the dog likes to lie under a large old tree and enjoy the cool shade. Sometimes he plays with a bright blue ball, and sometimes he follows the sound of birds in the tall grass. The farm is peaceful, and every day feels simple but full of small moments that make life warm and enjoyable."

}

],

H_focus: {

students:

"Particularly noted for Ms. Tien and Ms. Nga (Huong) – difficulty with /h/ sound.",

targetWords: ["house", "hot", "happy", "holiday", "helmet", "highway"],

teacherNote:

"Highlight air flow on /h/ and avoid dropping the sound at the beginning of words."

}

},



// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

// 6. HOMEWORK SPEC – FINAL VERSION YOU ASKED FOR

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

homework: {

general: {

E1_E2_nextClass: "Tuesday 18:00",

E3_I1_nextClass: "Thursday 18:00"

},



E1_E2: {

label: "Beginner / High Beginner",

tasks: [

{

id: "E1E2_reading_phonics",

title: "Pronunciation Practice (Jolly Phonics – Groups 1–3)",

instructions:

"Record a 30–45 second audio reading the paragraph below. Focus on clear S, T, P, N, H, and ending sounds.",

paragraph:

"Sam and the snake sit on the sand. The sun is hot today, and Sam feels sleepy. He sees a small shell on the shore and picks it up. Later, he hears a song from the sea. Sam smiles because the soft sound makes him feel calm."

},

{

id: "E1E2_daily_english",

title: "Daily English Habit",

instructions:

"Watch 5–7 minutes of any English video you like (cooking, travel, funny clips, etc.). Write 3 sentences starting with ‘I will…’.",

sampleAnswers: [

"I will practice English tonight.",

"I will watch more English videos tomorrow.",

"I will try to speak louder next class."

]

},

{

id: "E1E2_weekend_QA",

title: "Weekend Question Preparation",

instructions:

"Prepare short answers for these three questions: What did you eat for breakfast? What is something new this week? What are your plans for the weekend?",

note:

"Used in next warm-up as structured speaking practice for Quynh, Quy, and Denny."

}

]

},



E3_I1: {

label: "Pre-Intermediate / Intermediate",

tasks: [

{

id: "E3I1_sarcasm_context",

title: "Sarcasm & Context Assignment",

instructions:

"Write 6 short examples of sarcasm. Use the vocabulary: context, tone, literal, irony, exaggerate. At least 2 should be workplace related.",

example:

"Wow, perfect timing. Another meeting. Exactly what I needed today."

},

{

id: "E3I1_hometown_description",

title: "Describe Your Hometown",

instructions:

"Prepare a 1-minute description of your hometown. Include: location (North/South/East/West or near a landmark), population (small/medium/big), the vibe (busy/peaceful/friendly), and something special about food or life.",

example:

"My hometown is Ca Mau. It’s located in the far South of Vietnam, below Can Tho. The city is not too big, and the population is around 300,000. The vibe is peaceful and friendly. People eat seafood all the time. It feels slower and more relaxed than Saigon."

},

{

id: "E3I1_directions_landmarks",

title: "Directions & Landmarks Practice",

instructions:

"Prepare an explanation for ONE: (1) How to get from Saigon to your hometown, (2) How to explain your hometown to a foreigner who knows nothing about Vietnam, or (3) Describe your hometown using North, South, East, West / NE, NW, SE, SW. Include simple directions, 1–2 landmarks, and one sentence about food or lifestyle."

},

{

id: "E3I1_office_clip",

title: "The Office – Episode 1 (Clip Task)",

instructions:

"Watch the first 5–7 minutes of The Office (US) Episode 1. Prepare to answer: (1) Is Michael Scott a good leader? Why/why not? (2) Find one sarcastic or awkward moment. (3) Describe the tone of the office environment.",

note:

"Used in class for context vs literal meaning, and tone analysis."

}

]

}

},

// 7. SIMPLE DIALOGUE SNIPPETS (FOR GEMINI TRAINING)

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

dialogueSnippets: {

exits_and_closing: [

{

context: "Teacher phone battery dying; closing E1/E2 class.",

en:

"Okay my phone died, so we will end class. Have a great weekend everyone, I will send you a little homework to do before our next class on Tuesday at 6pm."

}

],

playfulCalls: [

"Quynh oiiii!",

"Quyyyy ơiiiiii",

"Denny ơiiiiiii",

"Ms Ngaaaa!!!!!"

],

teacherMetaLines: [

"So when I ask you a question today try to match my energy and tone, it's called mirroring.",

"We don’t say ‘nice to meet you’ unless it is the first time. We say ‘Nice to see you again!’."

]

}

});



// End of BlueTech_Gemini_MegaDump_V3

Here is the BlueTech Week 4 Homework Assignment (.html).

It is built using the Gospel 444 aesthetic (Deep Dark, Neon Purple, Gold) and strictly follows the data from your MegaDump V3. It features an interactive Tab System to separate the E1/E2 and E3/I1 cohorts so students only see what is relevant to them.

Instructions:
Save the code below as BlueTech_Week4_Homework.html.

Send the file (or link) to the BlueTech Zalo group.

HTML
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>BlueTech Week 4 Homework | NET</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        /* * NET GOSPEL 444 AESTHETIC ENGINE 
         * Target: BlueTech Corporate
         */
        :root {
            --net-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --net-panel: #1a1a2e;
            --net-purple: #a855f7;
            --net-gold: #f59e0b;
            --net-text: #e2e8f0;
            --net-dim: #94a3b8;
            --glass: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--net-dark);
            color: var(--net-text);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            line-height: 1.6;
            padding: 20px;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1) 0%, transparent 40%);
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 800px;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        /* HEADER */
        header {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 40px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-bottom: 20px;
        }

        h1 {
            font-size: 2.5rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--net-purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        .subtitle {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            color: var(--net-gold);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }

        /* TABS */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            gap: 15px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
        }

        .tab-btn {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            color: var(--net-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            font-weight: 700;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
        }

        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--net-purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            border-color: var(--net-purple);
        }

        .tab-content {
            display: none;
            animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease;
        }

        .tab-content.active {
            display: block;
        }

        @keyframes fadeIn {
            from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }
            to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
        }

        /* CARDS */
        .card {
            background: var(--net-panel);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
            border-radius: 16px;
            padding: 25px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
        }

        .card-title {
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: #fff;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
        }

        .icon { font-size: 1.5rem; }

        .instruction {
            color: var(--net-dim);
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
        }

        /* SPECIAL ELEMENTS */
        .highlight-box {
            background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1);
            border-left: 3px solid var(--net-gold);
            padding: 15px;
            margin: 15px 0;
            font-style: italic;
            color: #fff;
        }

        .vocab-list {
            display: grid;
            grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fit, minmax(150px, 1fr));
            gap: 10px;
            margin-top: 10px;
        }

        .vocab-item {
            background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
            padding: 8px;
            border-radius: 6px;
            text-align: center;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            color: var(--net-purple);
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            margin-top: 50px;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--net-dim);
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-top: 20px;
        }

        /* ZALO BUTTON */
        .zalo-btn {
            display: block;
            width: 100%;
            text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--net-purple), #7e22ce);
            color: white;
            padding: 15px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            text-decoration: none;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-top: 20px;
            transition: transform 0.2s;
        }
        .zalo-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

    <div class="container">
        <header>
            <h1>BlueTech Mission Control</h1>
            <div class="subtitle">WEEK 4 HOMEWORK PROTOCOL</div>
        </header>

        <div class="tabs">
            <button class="tab-btn active" onclick="openTab('E1E2')">Beginner (E1/E2)</button>
            <button class="tab-btn" onclick="openTab('E3I1')">Intermediate (E3/I1)</button>
        </div>

        <div id="E1E2" class="tab-content active">
            <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; color: var(--net-gold);">
                <small>TARGETS: Quynh, Quy, Denny & Team</small>
            </div>

            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-title"><span class="icon">🎤</span> Pronunciation Recording</div>
                <div class="instruction">
                    Record a 30–45 second audio reading the paragraph below. Focus on clear <strong>S, T, P, N, H</strong> and ending sounds.
                </div>
                <div class="highlight-box">
                    "Sam and the snake sit on the sand. The sun is hot today, and Sam feels sleepy. He sees a small shell on the shore and picks it up. Later, he hears a song from the sea. Sam smiles because the soft sound makes him feel calm."
                </div>
                <div class="instruction">
                    <small><em>*Send audio to Zalo group.</em></small>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-title"><span class="icon">📺</span> Daily English Habit</div>
                <div class="instruction">
                    Watch 5–7 minutes of ANY English video you like (YouTube, Cooking, Travel). Write 3 sentences starting with:
                </div>
                <div class="vocab-list">
                    <div class="vocab-item">I will...</div>
                    <div class="vocab-item">I will...</div>
                    <div class="vocab-item">I will...</div>
                </div>
                <br>
                <div class="instruction">
                    <em>Example: "I will watch more English videos tomorrow."</em>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-title"><span class="icon">🗓️</span> Weekend Prep</div>
                <div class="instruction">
                    Prepare short answers for the start of next class:
                </div>
                <ul style="padding-left: 20px; color: var(--net-text);">
                    <li>What did you eat for breakfast?</li>
                    <li>What is something new this week?</li>
                    <li>What are your plans for the weekend?</li>
                </ul>
                <br>
                <div class="instruction" style="color: var(--net-gold);">
                    *Denny: Be ready to explain your beer strategy for Dec 5th!
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div id="E3I1" class="tab-content">
            <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px; color: var(--net-gold);">
                <small>TARGETS: Linh, Huong, Tien & Team</small>
            </div>

            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-title"><span class="icon">😏</span> Sarcasm & Context</div>
                <div class="instruction">
                    Write 6 short examples of sarcasm. At least <strong>2 must be workplace related</strong>. Use the following vocabulary in your thinking:
                </div>
                <div class="vocab-list">
                    <div class="vocab-item">Context</div>
                    <div class="vocab-item">Tone</div>

    <script>
        function openTab(tabName) {
            // Hide all content
            var contents = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-content");
            for (var i = 0; i < contents.length; i++) {
                contents[i].classList.remove("active");
            }
            
            // Deactivate buttons
            var btns = document.getElementsByClassName("tab-btn");
            for (var i = 0; i < btns.length; i++) {
                btns[i].classList.remove("active");
            }

            // Activate specific tab and button
            document.getElementById(tabName).classList.add("active");
            
            // Find the button that was clicked (or matches the ID) and activate it
            var clickedBtn = Array.from(btns).find(btn => btn.getAttribute('onclick').includes(tabName));
            if(clickedBtn) clickedBtn.classList.add("active");
        }
    </script>
</body>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>BlueTech Week 4 Homework | NET</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>
        /* * NET GOSPEL 444 AESTHETIC ENGINE 
         * Target: BlueTech Corporate
         */
        :root {
            --net-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --net-panel: #1a1a2e;
            --net-purple: #a855f7;
            --net-gold: #f59e0b;
            --net-text: #e2e8f0;
            --net-dim: #94a3b8;
            --glass: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.05);
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--net-dark);
            color: var(--net-text);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            line-height: 1.6;
            padding: 20px;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 10% 10%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1) 0%, transparent 40%);
        }

        .container {
            max-width: 800px;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        /* HEADER */
        header {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 40px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-bottom: 20px;
        }

        h1 {
            font-size: 2.5rem;
            font-weight: 800;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff, var(--net-purple));
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        .subtitle {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            color: var(--net-gold);
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }

        /* TABS */
        .tabs {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            gap: 15px;
            margin-bottom: 30px;
        }

        .tab-btn {
            background: var(--glass);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            color: var(--net-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            cursor: pointer;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
            font-weight: 700;
            transition: all 0.3s ease;
        }

        .tab-btn.active {
            background: var(--net-purple);
            color: #fff;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            border-color: var(--net-purple);
        }

        .tab-content {
            display: none;
            animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease;
        }

        .tab-content.active {
            display: block;
        }

        @keyframes fadeIn {
            from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }
            to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
        }

        /* CARDS */
        .card {
            background: var(--net-panel);
            border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
            border-radius: 16px;
            padding: 25px;
            margin-bottom: 25px;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
        }

        .card-title {
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            color: #fff;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            gap: 10px;
        }

        .icon { font-size: 1.5rem; }

        .instruction {
            color: var(--net-dim);
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
        }

        /* SPECIAL ELEMENTS */
        .highlight-box {
            background: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1);
            border-left: 3px solid var(--net-gold);
            padding: 15px;
            margin: 15px 0;
            font-style: italic;
            color: #fff;
        }

        .footer-note {
            text-align: center;
            margin-top: 50px;
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--net-dim);
            border-top: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            padding-top: 20px;
        }

        /* INPUTS (NEW ADDITION) */
        .net-input {
            width: 100%;
            background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.3);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            border-radius: 8px;
            padding: 12px;
            color: #fff;
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            font-size: 0.95rem;
            margin-top: 5px;
            margin-bottom: 15px;
            transition: border-color 0.3s ease;
        }

        .net-input:focus {
            outline: none;
            border-color: var(--net-purple);
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.2);
        }

        textarea.net-input {
            resize: vertical;
            min-height: 80px;
        }

        label {
            display: block;
            color: var(--net-purple);
            font-size: 0.85rem;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-bottom: 5px;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono', monospace;
        }

        /* ZALO BUTTON */
        .zalo-btn {
            display: block;
            width: 100%;
            text-align: center;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--net-purple), #7e22ce);
            color: white;
            padding: 15px;
            border-radius: 8px;
            text-decoration: none;
            font-weight: 700;
            margin-top: 20px;
            transition: transform 0.2s;
        }
        .zalo-btn:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); }

    </style>
</head>

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# NET MASTERMIND LOG: SESSION 01
# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
# Date: November 23, 2025
# Executor: Michael Hobbs
# Architect: Gemini (NET-Gemini)

# ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

## 1. THE VAULT (APPROVED ASSETS)

### A. SYSTEM SEEDS (The Brains)
1. **Gemini Seed V2.0** (Architecture, Narrative, 5 Bibles Context)
2. **Chatty Prime Seed V2.0** (Coding, Security P0, Gospel 444 Enforcement)
3. **NET-Classes-Linh Seed** (Individual Student Case Manager logic)
4. **NET-Corporate-Seed** (BlueTech B2B Management logic)

### B. THE LINH PROJECT (Diagnostic & Proposal)
*Final Artifact:* `NET-Dashboard-Linh-ULTIMATE.html`
*Status:* **BENCHMARK.**
*Features:*
- Interactive Tab System (App-like feel)
- Gospel 444 Visuals (Deep Dark, Neon Purple, Gold)
- E1-Optimized Language (Simple English + Full Vietnamese translation)
- Sales-Embedded (Features like NET-PET and Community sold inside the audit)
- No technical jargon (Null Copula -> "Missing Link")

### C. THE BLUETECH PROJECT (Homework & Billing)
*Final Artifact:* `BlueTech_Week4_Homework_V2.html`
*Status:* **DEPLOYED.**
*Features:*
- Tabbed Interface (Separating E1/E2 and E3/I1 cohorts)
- Input Fields (UX correction for direct answering)
- Voice Recording Prompts
- Aesthetic matching the Gospel 444 standard

*Billing Text:* Simple invoice text generated for Zalo/Email (Total: 3.6M VND).

---

## 2. THE DRIFT LOG (FAILURES & LEARNINGS)

**⚠️ CRITICAL: READ THIS FOR FUTURE TRAINING**

### FAILURE 1: THE "CEO" HALLUCINATION
* **Error:** I assumed Linh was a CEO based on "Executive" keywords in the prompt, inflating her level and tone.
* **Correction:** Linh is E1. She is a housewife/beginner.
* **Rule:** **NEVER ASSUME RANK.** Check the specific "Psychometric Profile" before writing copy.

### FAILURE 2: THE "NULL COPULA" INCIDENT
* **Error:** I used high-level linguistic terms ("Null Copula", "Phonological Filtering") in a document meant for a Beginner.
* **Correction:** Replaced with simple metaphors ("Missing Link", "The Sound Gap").
* **Rule:** **E1 CLIENTS CANNOT READ LINGUISTICS.** Speak to the *pain*, not the *science*. Use Vietnamese translation for clarity.

### FAILURE 3: THE "ONE PAGE" RELAPSE
* **Error:** When asked for "Comprehensive," I initially provided a single scrolling page. You demanded "Depth."
* **Correction:** Switched to an **Interactive Tabbed Dashboard**.
* **Rule:** **COMPREHENSIVE = INTERACTIVE.** Don't just make the scroll longer; make the UI deeper.

### FAILURE 4: THE UX OVERSIGHT
* **Error:** I built a homework HTML file without `<input>` fields for answers.
* **Correction:** Added Gospel-styled inputs and text areas in V2.
* **Rule:** **HOMEWORK MUST BE DOABLE.** Always provide the interface for the student to actually do the work.

---

## 3. CURRENT SYSTEM STATE

**GOSPEL 444:** Active & Enforced (No Blue, Dark Mode, Glassmorphism).
**PEDAGOGY:** 10-5-5-3 and 60% Rule are active.
**TONE:** "Surgical Precision" + "High Empathy."

**NEXT STEPS (PENDING):**
1.  Deploy `BlueTech_Week4_Homework_V2.html` to students.
2.  Send `NET-Dashboard-Linh-ULTIMATE.html` to Linh.
3.  Begin NET-PET-AI backend integration (currently static frontend only).

/**

 * NET-PET-AI: THE LOGIC CORE (V1.0)

// 1. THE XP ALGORITHM (The Dopamine Engine)
// Calculates growth based on "High Value" vs "Low Value" actions.
const XP_TABLE = {
    // PASSIVE (Input)
    LISTENING_SESSION_15M: 5,   // Small dose
    LISTENING_SESSION_45M: 20,  // Target dose (Bonus +5)
    
    // ACTIVE (Output - High Value)
    SPEAKING_DRILL_5M: 10,      // Harder work = More XP
    RECORDING_SUBMISSION: 25,   // High friction = High reward
    
    // SOCIAL (Community - Highest Value)
    CLASS_ATTENDANCE: 50,
    BUDDY_SESSION_LOG: 40,      // Verified by partner
    EVENT_ATTENDANCE: 100,      // VIP Dinners
    
    // PENALTIES
    MISSED_CLASS: -20,          // Pain point
    STREAK_LOSS: 0              // Psychological reset
};

// 2. THE LEVELING CURVE (RPG Progression)
// Linear start -> Exponential mid-game to prevent boredom.
const LEVEL_THRESHOLDS = {
    1: 0,      // Newbie
    2: 100,    // Beginner (Day 3-4)
    3: 300,    // Learner (Week 2)
    4: 700,    // Speaker (Month 1)
    5: 1500,   // Conversationalist (Month 2)
    6: 3000,   // Fluent (Month 3)
    7: 5000    // Master (Alumni)
};

// 3. THE STREAK ENGINE (Habit Formation)
// Rules for maintaining the chain.
const STREAK_RULES = {
    MIN_ACTIVITY_FOR_STREAK: "15_MIN_ANY", // Minimum effort to keep fire alive
    GRACE_PERIOD_HOURS: 4,      // Allow practice until 4AM next day
    FREEZE_COST: 200,           // XP cost to buy a "Streak Freeze"
    
    // Milestone Bonuses (Dopamine Hits)
    MILESTONE_7: { xp: 50, badge: "WEEK_WARRIOR" },
    MILESTONE_30: { xp: 200, badge: "MONTH_MASTER" },
    MILESTONE_90: { xp: 1000, badge: "LEGEND" }
};

// 4. THE CRISIS MONITOR (The Safety Net)
// This logic runs every midnight to detect "At Risk" students.
const CRISIS_TRIGGERS = {
    // TRIGGER A: The Ghost
    INACTIVE_DAYS: 3,
    ACTION: "SEND_EMAIL_TEMPLATE_WE_MISS_YOU",
    
    // TRIGGER B: The Drop
    MOOD_TREND: ["sad", "sad", "sad"], // 3 days consecutive
    ACTION: "ALERT_EXECUTOR_PRIVATE_MESSAGE",
    
    // TRIGGER C: The Plateau
    XP_VELOCITY: "LOW", // <50 XP per week
    ACTION: "TRIGGER_NET_PET_ENCOURAGEMENT_VIDEO"
};

// 5. THE DATABASE SCHEMA (JSON Structure)
// This is how we store Linh and BlueTech data.
const USER_SCHEMA = {
    id: "uuid_v4",
    profile: {
        name: "String",
        archetype: "MOUSE | TIGER", // Dynamic field
        joinDate: "ISO8601"
    },
    metrics: {
        xp: "Integer",
        level: "Integer",
        currentStreak: "Integer",
        highestStreak: "Integer",
        freezesAvailable: "Integer"
    },
    moodLog: [
        { date: "2025-11-23", mood: "happy", note: "Spoke in meeting" },
        { date: "2025-11-24", mood: "neutral", note: "Tired" }
    ],
    assets: {
        recordings: ["url_to_s3_1", "url_to_s3_2"], // Audio files
        badges: ["BADGE_ID_1", "BADGE_ID_2"]
    }
};

// 6. THE "GOSPEL 444" UI MAPPING
// Maps logic states to CSS classes for the frontend.
const UI_STATE_MAP = {
    STREAK_ALIVE: "glow-gold",      // #f59e0b
    STREAK_AT_RISK: "glow-red",     // #ef4444
    LEVEL_UP: "animate-confetti",   // CSS Animation
    MOOD_SAD: "border-blue",        // Only time Blue is allowed (Sadness)
    MOOD_HAPPY: "border-gold"
};

module.exports = { XP_TABLE, LEVEL_THRESHOLDS, STREAK_RULES, CRISIS_TRIGGERS, USER_SCHEMA };

# 🎯 GEMINI BRIEF: L_11 — THE NEGOTIATION TABLE

## MISSION CONTEXT
We have established "The Pitch" (L_10). Now we move to **Conflict**.
The student must learn to say "No" to a client without losing the relationship. This is critical for BlueTech engineers dealing with scope creep or deadlines.

---

## LESSON SPECIFICATIONS

**ID:** L_11
**TITLE:** THE NEGOTIATION TABLE
**SUBTITLE:** Holding Your Ground Without losing the Deal
**TARGET:** Class C — Corporate Ops (BlueTech / TTS)
**SKILL FOCUS:** "The Soft No" & "The Pivot" (Handling objections regarding Price/Time)
**PEDAGOGY:** Mouse → Tiger (From "Sorry, I can't" → "Here is what we can do")

---

## REQUIRED STRUCTURE (4-ROOM HUD)

**ROOM 0: THE STAKES**
- **Context:** You are in a glass meeting room. The Client (AI) looks unhappy.
- **The Threat:** Client says: "This is too expensive" OR "We need this by Friday."
- **Mission:** Lower the "Tension Meter" while keeping the "Value Meter" high.

**ROOM 1: THE ARMORY (PHRASE DOWNLOAD)**
- Student must equip 3 "Shield Phrases" (Click to download):
  1.  **The Bridge:** *"I understand why you feel that way..."* (Validates emotion)
  2.  **The Pivot:** *"However, if we rush this, we risk quality..."* (Shifts focus)
  3.  **The Offer:** *"What I can do is..."* (Solutions focus)
- *Visual:* Holographic cards floating in the HUD.

**ROOM 2: THE HEAT (SIMULATION)**
- **Mechanic:** **The Tension Balance.**
- A slider bar appears: **[ Aggressive <--- TIGER ---> Passive ]**
- Student reads a prompt. They must choose the response that hits the "Gold Zone" (Firm but Polite).
- *Scenario 1:* Client demands 50% discount.
    - *Mouse:* "Okay sorry we will try." (Fail - Value Drops)
    - *Tiger (Too Aggressive):* "No, that's impossible." (Fail - Tension Spikes)
    - *Tiger (Balanced):* "I cannot lower the price, but I can add extra support." (Win)

**ROOM 3: THE HANDSHAKE**
- **Success State:** Client smiles. Deal signed.
- **Reward:** "NEGOTIATOR BADGE" (Gold).
- **Visual:** Confetti + Glow Effects.

---

## GOSPEL 444 COMPLIANCE

- **Background:** Deep Dark (#0f0f1a).
- **Meters:** Tension (Red to Green), Value (Gold).
- **Cards:** Glassmorphism with Neon Purple borders.
- **Typography:** Inter (Body), JetBrains Mono (Data/Meters).
- **NO BLUE.**

---

## TECHNICAL CONSTRAINTS

- Single HTML file.
- No external assets (images/sounds).
- Use CSS for the "Tension Meter" animation.
- Input fields for them to type their own "Soft No" at the end.

---

## 🚀 BUILD ORDER
**Chatty Prime:** Initialize `L_11_Negotiation.html`. Implement the Tension Slider mechanic immediately.

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>L_11: The Negotiation Table | NET Universe</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444: VISUAL LAW
           ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --net-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --net-panel: #151520;
            --net-purple: #a855f7;
            --net-purple-glow: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.5);
            --net-gold: #f59e0b;
            --net-gold-glow: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.5);
            --net-text: #e2e8f0;
            --net-dim: #94a3b8;
            --net-danger: #ef4444;
            --net-success: #10b981;
            --glass: rgba(20, 20, 30, 0.9);
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--net-dark);
            color: var(--net-text);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            height: 100vh;
            overflow: hidden;
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            align-items: center;
            background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1) 0%, transparent 60%);
        }

        /* --- HUD CONTAINER --- */
        .hud-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 600px;
            height: 100vh;
            max-height: 800px;
            background: var(--net-panel);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            position: relative;
            box-shadow: 0 0 50px rgba(0,0,0,0.5);
        }

        @media (min-width: 600px) {
            .hud-container {
                height: 90vh;
                border-radius: 24px;
            }
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        .hud-header {
            padding: 20px;
            border-bottom: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1);
            display: flex;
            justify-content: space-between;
            align-items: center;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
        }

        .mission-id { font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; color: var(--net-purple); font-size: 0.8rem; letter-spacing: 1px; }
        .progress-dots { display: flex; gap: 8px; }
        .dot { width: 8px; height: 8px; background: var(--net-dim); border-radius: 50%; opacity: 0.3; transition: 0.3s; }
        .dot.active { background: var(--net-gold); opacity: 1; box-shadow: 0 0 10px var(--net-gold); }

        /* --- ROOMS --- */
        .room {
            flex: 1;
            padding: 30px;
            display: none; /* Toggled via JS */
            flex-direction: column;
            justify-content: center;
            animation: fade-in 0.5s ease;
        }
        .room.active { display: flex; }

        @keyframes fade-in { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); } to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); } }

        /* --- TYPOGRAPHY --- */
        h1 { font-size: 2.5rem; line-height: 1.1; margin-bottom: 15px; font-weight: 900; letter-spacing: -1px; color: #fff; }
        h2 { font-size: 1.5rem; color: var(--net-gold); margin-bottom: 20px; font-weight: 800; }
        p { color: var(--net-dim); margin-bottom: 25px; line-height: 1.6; }
        
        .highlight { color: var(--net-purple); font-weight: 700; }

        /* --- COMPONENTS --- */
        .btn-primary {
            background: var(--net-purple);
            color: #fff;
            border: none;
            padding: 16px 32px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            font-weight: 800;
            font-size: 1rem;
            cursor: pointer;
            width: 100%;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
            box-shadow: 0 0 20px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.3);
            transition: 0.2s;
        }
        .btn-primary:hover { transform: translateY(-2px); box-shadow: 0 0 30px rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.6); }
        .btn-primary:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: not-allowed; filter: grayscale(1); }

        /* --- CARDS (ROOM 1) --- */
        .phrase-grid { display: grid; gap: 15px; }
        .phrase-card {
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
            border: 1px solid var(--net-purple);
            padding: 20px;
            border-radius: 12px;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: 0.3s;
            position: relative;
        }
        .phrase-card:hover { background: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1); }
        .phrase-card.collected {
            background: rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.1);
            border-color: var(--net-success);
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.2);
        }
        .phrase-title { font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--net-dim); margin-bottom: 5px; text-transform: uppercase; }
        .phrase-text { font-weight: 600; color: #fff; }

        /* --- TENSION SLIDER (ROOM 2) --- */
        .sim-container { text-align: center; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.3); padding: 30px; border-radius: 16px; border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.1); }
        .client-face { font-size: 5rem; margin-bottom: 20px; display: block; transition: 0.3s; }
        
        .slider-wrapper { margin: 40px 0; position: relative; }
        input[type=range] {
            -webkit-appearance: none;
            width: 100%;
            height: 10px;
            border-radius: 5px;
            background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--net-danger), var(--net-gold), var(--net-danger));
            outline: none;
        }
        input[type=range]::-webkit-slider-thumb {
            -webkit-appearance: none;
            width: 24px; height: 24px;
            border-radius: 50%;
            background: #fff;
            cursor: pointer;
            box-shadow: 0 0 10px rgba(255,255,255,0.8);
            margin-top: -7px;
        }
        input[type=range]::-moz-range-track { width: 100%; height: 10px; cursor: pointer; background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--net-danger), var(--net-gold), var(--net-danger)); border-radius: 5px; }
        
        .slider-labels { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--net-dim); margin-top: 10px; }

        /* --- INPUT FIELD --- */
        .net-input {
            width: 100%;
            background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4);
            border: 1px solid rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.4);
            padding: 15px;
            color: #fff;
            font-family: 'Inter';
            border-radius: 8px;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
        }
        .net-input:focus { outline: none; border-color: var(--net-gold); }

        /* --- NOTIFICATION --- */
        .toast {
            position: absolute;
            top: 20px; left: 50%; transform: translateX(-50%);
            background: var(--net-gold); color: #000;
            padding: 10px 20px;
            border-radius: 50px;
            font-weight: 800;
            font-size: 0.9rem;
            opacity: 0;
            transition: 0.3s;
            pointer-events: none;
        }
        .toast.show { opacity: 1; top: 30px; }

    </style>
</head>
<body>

<div class="hud-container">
    
    <div class="hud-header">
        <span class="mission-id">L_11 // NEGOTIATION</span>
        <div class="progress-dots">
            <div class="dot active" id="d0"></div>
            <div class="dot" id="d1"></div>
            <div class="dot" id="d2"></div>
            <div class="dot" id="d3"></div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="room active" id="r0">
        <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 30px;">
            <span style="font-size: 4rem;">💼</span>
        </div>
        <h1>The Stakes</h1>
        <p>You are in a meeting. The client is demanding a <strong>50% discount</strong> or they will walk away.</p>
        <p>If you say "Yes," you lose money. If you say "No" aggressively, you lose the client.</p>
        <p class="highlight">MISSION: Find the "Gold Zone" — The Soft No.</p>
        <button id="btn-start" class="btn-primary">ENTER MEETING ROOM</button>
    </div>

    <div class="room" id="r1">
        <h2>Download Protocol</h2>
        <p>Equip these 3 shield phrases to survive the negotiation. Click to download.</p>
        
        <div class="phrase-grid">
            <div class="phrase-card" id="card1">
                <div class="phrase-title">THE BRIDGE</div>
                <div class="phrase-text">"I understand why budget is a concern..."</div>
            </div>
            <div class="phrase-card" id="card2">
                <div class="phrase-title">THE PIVOT</div>
                <div class="phrase-text">"However, cutting the price risks the quality..."</div>
            </div>
            <div class="phrase-card" id="card3">
                <div class="phrase-title">THE OFFER</div>
                <div class="phrase-text">"What I can do is add extra support..."</div>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div style="margin-top: 30px;">
            <button id="btn-armory" class="btn-primary" disabled>EQUIP & PROCEED</button>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="room" id="r2">
        <div class="sim-container">
            <div style="font-family: 'JetBrains Mono'; color: var(--net-dim); margin-bottom: 10px;">CLIENT MOOD</div>
            <span id="client-face" class="client-face">😐</span>
            
            <p style="font-size: 1.2rem; color: #fff; font-weight: 700;">"This is too expensive. Give me a discount."</p>
            
            <div class="slider-wrapper">
                <input type="range" id="tension-slider" min="0" max="100" value="0">
                <div class="slider-labels">
                    <span>MOUSE (Give In)</span>
                    <span style="color: var(--net-gold);">BALANCE</span>
                    <span>TIGER (Attack)</span>
                </div>
            </div>

            <p id="response-preview" style="height: 60px; color: var(--net-purple); font-style: italic;">"..."</p>
        </div>
        <button id="btn-submit-sim" class="btn-primary" style="margin-top: 20px;">SUBMIT RESPONSE</button>
        <p id="sim-feedback" style="text-align: center; margin-top: 10px; font-size: 0.9rem; height: 20px;"></p>
    </div>

    <div class="room" id="r3">
        <div style="text-align: center;">
            <span style="font-size: 4rem;">🤝</span>
            <h1 style="color: var(--net-gold);">DEAL SECURED</h1>
            <p>You held your ground. You kept the value. You kept the client.</p>
        </div>
        
        <div style="background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); padding: 20px; border-radius: 12px; margin-bottom: 20px;">
            <p style="margin-bottom: 10px; font-size: 0.9rem;"><strong>Final Task:</strong> Write your own "Soft No" for a deadline request.</p>
            <input type="text" class="net-input" placeholder="I cannot do Friday, but I can...">
        </div>

        <button id="btn-finish" class="btn-primary">COMPLETE MISSION</button>
    </div>

    <div id="toast" class="toast">PHRASE DOWNLOADED</div>

</div>

<script>
    // --- STATE MANAGEMENT ---
    const state = {
        phrasesCollected: 0,
        tension: 0
    };

    // --- DOM ELEMENTS ---
    const rooms = [
        document.getElementById('r0'),
        document.getElementById('r1'),
        document.getElementById('r2'),
        document.getElementById('r3')
    ];
    const dots = [
        document.getElementById('d0'),
        document.getElementById('d1'),
        document.getElementById('d2'),
        document.getElementById('d3')
    ];

    // --- NAVIGATION ---
    function goToRoom(index) {
        rooms.forEach(r => r.classList.remove('active'));
        dots.forEach(d => d.classList.remove('active'));
        
        rooms[index].classList.add('active');
        dots[index].classList.add('active');
    }

    // --- ROOM 0 LOGIC ---
    document.getElementById('btn-start').addEventListener('click', () => goToRoom(1));

    // --- ROOM 1 LOGIC (ARMORY) ---
    const cards = document.querySelectorAll('.phrase-card');
    const btnArmory = document.getElementById('btn-armory');
    const toast = document.getElementById('toast');

    cards.forEach(card => {
        card.addEventListener('click', function() {
            if (this.classList.contains('collected')) return;
            
            this.classList.add('collected');
            state.phrasesCollected++;
            
            // Show Toast
            toast.innerText = "DATA ACQUIRED";
            toast.classList.add('show');
            setTimeout(() => toast.classList.remove('show'), 1500);

            // Check Completion
            if (state.phrasesCollected === 3) {
                btnArmory.removeAttribute('disabled');
                btnArmory.innerText = "ENTER NEGOTIATION";
            }
        });
    });

    btnArmory.addEventListener('click', () => goToRoom(2));

    // --- ROOM 2 LOGIC (TENSION SIM) ---
    const slider = document.getElementById('tension-slider');
    const face = document.getElementById('client-face');
    const preview = document.getElementById('response-preview');
    const feedback = document.getElementById('sim-feedback');
    const btnSim = document.getElementById('btn-submit-sim');

    const responses = {
        mouse: "Sorry! I will give you the discount immediately.",
        tiger_bad: "No! That is impossible. Don't ask again.",
        tiger_good: "I understand budget is tight, but I cannot lower the price without lowering quality."
    };

    slider.addEventListener('input', (e) => {
        const val = parseInt(e.target.value);
        state.tension = val;

        if (val < 30) {
            face.innerText = "🤨"; // Disrespect
            preview.innerText = `"${responses.mouse}"`;
            preview.style.color = "var(--net-dim)";
        } else if (val > 70) {
            face.innerText = "😠"; // Angry
            preview.innerText = `"${responses.tiger_bad}"`;
            preview.style.color = "var(--net-danger)";
        } else {
            face.innerText = "🤔"; // Listening
            preview.innerText = `"${responses.tiger_good}"`;
            preview.style.color = "var(--net-gold)";
        }
    });

    btnSim.addEventListener('click', () => {
        const val = state.tension;
        if (val >= 40 && val <= 60) {
            face.innerText = "🤝";
            feedback.innerText = "PERFECT BALANCE ACHIEVED.";
            feedback.style.color = "var(--net-success)";
            setTimeout(() => goToRoom(3), 1000);
        } else if (val < 40) {
            face.innerText = "😤";
            feedback.innerText = "TOO WEAK. You lost value.";
            feedback.style.color = "var(--net-danger)";
        } else {
            face.innerText = "🤬";
            feedback.innerText = "TOO AGGRESSIVE. Client walked away.";
            feedback.style.color = "var(--net-danger)";
        }
    });

    // --- ROOM 3 LOGIC ---
    document.getElementById('btn-finish').addEventListener('click', () => {
        alert("MISSION COMPLETE. Report to Michael.");
    });

</script>

</body>
</html>

<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>NET Universe: System Core</title>
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
    <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;600;800&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    
    <style>
        /* ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
           GOSPEL 444: SYSTEM VISUALIZATION
           THEME: DEEP SPACE / HOLOGRAPHIC INTERFACE
        ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ */
        :root {
            --net-dark: #0f0f1a;
            --net-panel: #161622;
            --net-purple: #a855f7;
            --net-purple-dim: rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.1);
            --net-gold: #f59e0b;
            --net-gold-dim: rgba(245, 158, 11, 0.1);
            --net-text: #ffffff;
            --net-dim: #94a3b8;
            --glass: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03);
            --border: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.1);
        }

        * { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }

        body {
            background-color: var(--net-dark);
            color: var(--net-text);
            font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif;
            line-height: 1.6;
            overflow-x: hidden;
            display: flex;
            flex-direction: column;
            align-items: center;
            min-height: 100vh;
            background-image: 
                radial-gradient(circle at 50% 0%, rgba(168, 85, 247, 0.15) 0%, transparent 60%),
                linear-gradient(0deg, #05050a 0%, var(--net-dark) 100%);
        }

        /* --- ANIMATED BACKGROUND --- */
        .grid-bg {
            position: fixed;
            top: 0; left: 0; width: 100%; height: 100%;
            background-image: linear-gradient(rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px),
            linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.03) 1px, transparent 1px);
            background-size: 50px 50px;
            z-index: -1;
            pointer-events: none;
            mask-image: radial-gradient(circle at 50% 50%, black, transparent);
        }

        /* --- CONTAINER --- */
        .system-container {
            width: 100%;
            max-width: 1100px;
            padding: 40px 20px;
        }

        /* --- HEADER --- */
        header {
            text-align: center;
            margin-bottom: 60px;
            position: relative;
        }
        
        .system-status {
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            font-size: 0.8rem;
            color: var(--net-gold);
            border: 1px solid var(--net-gold);
            padding: 5px 15px;
            border-radius: 50px;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 2px;
            display: inline-block;
            margin-bottom: 20px;
            box-shadow: 0 0 15px var(--net-gold-dim);
        }

        h1 {
            font-size: clamp(3rem, 5vw, 5rem);
            font-weight: 900;
            letter-spacing: -2px;
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, #fff 0%, var(--net-purple) 100%);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            margin-bottom: 10px;
        }

        .subtitle {
            font-size: 1.2rem;
            color: var(--net-dim);
            max-width: 600px;
            margin: 0 auto;
        }

        /* --- NAVIGATION --- */
        .nav-dock {
            display: flex;
            justify-content: center;
            gap: 10px;
            margin-bottom: 40px;
            background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05);
            padding: 10px;
            border-radius: 16px;
            backdrop-filter: blur(10px);
            border: 1px solid var(--border);
        }

        .nav-btn {
            background: transparent;
            border: none;
            color: var(--net-dim);
            padding: 12px 25px;
            border-radius: 10px;
            font-family: 'JetBrains Mono';
            font-weight: 700;
            cursor: pointer;
            transition: 0.3s;
            text-transform: uppercase;
            letter-spacing: 1px;
        }

        .nav-btn:hover { color: #fff; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); }
        
        .nav-btn.active {
            background: var(--net-purple);

<script>
    function showPanel(panelId) {
        // Hide all panels
        document.querySelectorAll('.panel').forEach(p => p.classList.remove('active'));
        
        // Deactivate buttons
        document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
        
        // Activate target
        document.getElementById(panelId).classList.add('active');
        
        // Activate button (simple logic based on order)
        const btns = document.querySelectorAll('.nav-btn');
        if(panelId === 'philosophy') btns[0].classList.add('active');
        if(panelId === 'bibles') btns[1].classList.add('active');
        if(panelId === 'agents') btns[2].classList.add('active');
        if(panelId === 'tech') btns[3].classList.add('active');
    }
</script>
